**最后尝试回答：** 概述一场冲突的报道：起因、参与者、威胁、应对、后果和妥协。

### 词群 18.01｜冲突的规模与任务 {#group-18-01}

**串联方式：** 从暴力、冲突、突袭到战役、战斗和斗争，再说明任务；enormity 常强调罪恶或严重性。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">violence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-001" aria-label="定位 violence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-001" data-mark-label="violence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="violence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="violence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 violence" title="在有道查询 violence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.暴力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The raid exposed the enormity of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>violence</strong></a> caused by the conflict.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">突袭揭示了冲突造成的暴力的骇人程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Only after the raid did observers understand the enormity of the conflict and its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>violence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">突袭后，观察者才理解冲突及其暴力的严重程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Survivors described the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>violence</strong></a> of the raid and the enormity of their losses during the conflict.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">幸存者描述了突袭的暴力，以及冲突中损失的巨大程度。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">domestic violence</span> · 家庭暴力</p>

</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.战役；运动；v.参加运动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rescue mission became a struggle for survival as the battle disrupted the relief <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=campaign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>campaign</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">战斗打乱救援行动，使救援任务变成了一场求生斗争。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=campaign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>campaign</strong></a> to rebuild the town followed the battle, but each mission faced a struggle for funding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">战斗后，重建城镇的行动开始了，但每项任务都面临筹资困难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The documentary follows a medical mission through a battle and the subsequent struggle to sustain its vaccination <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=campaign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>campaign</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪录片跟随医疗队经历战斗，以及此后维持疫苗接种行动的艰难过程。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A campaign to rebuild the town followed the battle, but each mission faced a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=struggle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>struggle</strong></a> for funding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">战斗后，重建城镇的行动开始了，但每项任务都面临筹资困难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The documentary follows a medical mission through a battle and the subsequent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=struggle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>struggle</strong></a> to sustain its vaccination campaign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪录片跟随医疗队经历战斗，以及此后维持疫苗接种行动的艰难过程。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 18.02｜爆炸、火焰与光亮 {#group-18-02}

**串联方式：** 由爆炸和轰炸进入火药、火花、火焰与闪光；twinkle、glitter 等光亮词不必含战争义。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">explosion<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-008" aria-label="定位 explosion">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-008" data-mark-label="explosion" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="explosion：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="explosion：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 explosion" title="在有道查询 explosion">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.爆发；迸发；爆炸；激增</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bomb <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explosion</strong></a> produced a blast as aircraft continued to bombard the outskirts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞机继续轰炸郊区时，炸弹爆炸产生了冲击波。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents heard an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explosion</strong></a> and felt the blast before planes returned to bombard the area with another bomb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民听到爆炸并感到冲击波，随后飞机返回，向该地区投下另一枚炸弹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit explains how a bomb creates an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explosion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explosion</strong></a> and blast, alongside records of orders to bombard the port.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览解释炸弹如何产生爆炸与冲击波，并展示了炮击港口的命令记录。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gas explosion</span> · 燃气爆炸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shelter</a></span> · 避难所</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evacuation</a></span> · 撤离</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bomb explosion produced a blast as aircraft continued to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bombard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bombard</strong></a> the outskirts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞机继续轰炸郊区时，炸弹爆炸产生了冲击波。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents heard an explosion and felt the blast before planes returned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bombard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bombard</strong></a> the area with another bomb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民听到爆炸并感到冲击波，随后飞机返回，向该地区投下另一枚炸弹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit explains how a bomb creates an explosion and blast, alongside records of orders to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bombard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bombard</strong></a> the port.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览解释炸弹如何产生爆炸与冲击波，并展示了炮击港口的命令记录。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-012">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)爆裂；(使)胀开；n.迸发；爆裂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A spark reached the gunpowder, producing a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burst</strong></a> of flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一粒火星接触到火药，猛然窜起火焰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep gunpowder away from a spark or naked flame to prevent a sudden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burst</strong></a> of fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">让火药远离火星或明火，防止火焰突然爆发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical display used lighting to suggest a spark, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burst</strong></a> of gunpowder and a rising flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史展览用灯光模拟火星、火药爆发及升起的火焰。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a burst of light</span> · 一阵亮光</p>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spark</strong></a> reached the gunpowder, producing a burst of flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一粒火星接触到火药，猛然窜起火焰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep gunpowder away from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spark</strong></a> or naked flame to prevent a sudden burst of fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">让火药远离火星或明火，防止火焰突然爆发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical display used lighting to suggest a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spark</strong></a>, a burst of gunpowder and a rising flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史展览用灯光模拟火星、火药爆发及升起的火焰。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a spark of fire</span> · 火花</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ignition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ignition</a></span> · 点火</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fire%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fire safety</a></span> · 消防安全</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">twinkle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-014" aria-label="定位 twinkle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-014" data-mark-label="twinkle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="twinkle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="twinkle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 twinkle" title="在有道查询 twinkle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.闪烁，闪耀；v.闪烁，闪耀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Stars <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twinkle</strong></a> above the sea as waves glitter beneath the glow of a distant flare.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">星星在海面上空闪烁，远处信号弹的光映得海浪发亮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We saw the flare glow, the water glitter and the lighthouse lights <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twinkle</strong></a> in the distance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看到信号弹发光、水面闪耀，远处灯塔的灯闪烁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The painting captures a flare and its glow, with a glitter of reflections beneath stars that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twinkle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twinkle</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">画作描绘了信号弹及其光芒，闪烁星空下的水面泛着粼光。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stars twinkle</span> · 星星闪烁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反光</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=illumination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">illumination</a></span> · 照明</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">gunpowder<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-015" aria-label="定位 gunpowder">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-015" data-mark-label="gunpowder" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gunpowder：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gunpowder：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gunpowder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gunpowder" title="在有道查询 gunpowder">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-015</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.火药</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A spark reached the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gunpowder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gunpowder</strong></a>, producing a burst of flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一粒火星接触到火药，猛然窜起火焰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gunpowder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gunpowder</strong></a> away from a spark or naked flame to prevent a sudden burst of fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">让火药远离火星或明火，防止火焰突然爆发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical display used lighting to suggest a spark, a burst of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gunpowder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gunpowder</strong></a> and a rising flame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史展览用灯光模拟火星、火药爆发及升起的火焰。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep gunpowder away from a spark or naked <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flame</strong></a> to prevent a sudden burst of fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">让火药远离火星或明火，防止火焰突然爆发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical display used lighting to suggest a spark, a burst of gunpowder and a rising <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flame</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史展览用灯光模拟火星、火药爆发及升起的火焰。</p></li>

</ol>

### 词群 18.03｜武器、弹药与目标 {#group-18-03}

**串联方式：** 从重型装备到手持武器和子弹，再看目标与偏转；作为报道阅读中的识别词群。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">tank<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-016" aria-label="定位 tank">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-016" data-mark-label="tank" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tank：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tank：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tank&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tank" title="在有道查询 tank">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.坦克；罐，槽，缸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum displays a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tank&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tank</strong></a>, a cannon and a missile to illustrate changes in weapon design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆展出坦克、火炮和导弹，以说明武器设计的变化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tank&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tank</strong></a> or cannon, a guided missile can be a weapon controlled from far away.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与坦克或火炮不同，制导导弹可成为远程控制的武器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The treaty classifies each weapon, including the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tank&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tank</strong></a>, the cannon and the missile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">条约对各类武器作了分类，包括坦克、火炮和导弹。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">pistol<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-020" aria-label="定位 pistol">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-020" data-mark-label="pistol" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pistol：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pistol：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pistol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pistol" title="在有道查询 pistol">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-020</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.手枪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The test examined whether a barrier could deflect a bullet from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pistol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pistol</strong></a> or rifle away from the target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试考察屏障能否让手枪或步枪子弹偏离目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the museum, a diagram shows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pistol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pistol</strong></a>, a rifle and the path a shield might deflect a bullet from its target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的示意图展示了手枪、步枪，以及盾牌可能使子弹偏离目标的路径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers used a protected target to compare how armour could deflect a bullet fired from a rifle or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pistol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pistol</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者用有防护的靶子，比较装甲如何偏转步枪或手枪射出的子弹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a service pistol</span> · 制式手枪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballistics</a></span> · 弹道学</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protective%20barrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protective barrier</a></span> · 防护屏障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The test examined whether a barrier could deflect a bullet from a pistol or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rifle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rifle</strong></a> away from the target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试考察屏障能否让手枪或步枪子弹偏离目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the museum, a diagram shows a pistol, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rifle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rifle</strong></a> and the path a shield might deflect a bullet from its target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的示意图展示了手枪、步枪，以及盾牌可能使子弹偏离目标的路径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers used a protected target to compare how armour could deflect a bullet fired from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rifle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rifle</strong></a> or pistol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者用有防护的靶子，比较装甲如何偏转步枪或手枪射出的子弹。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The test examined whether a barrier could deflect a bullet from a pistol or rifle away from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=target&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>target</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试考察屏障能否让手枪或步枪子弹偏离目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the museum, a diagram shows a pistol, a rifle and the path a shield might deflect a bullet from its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=target&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>target</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的示意图展示了手枪、步枪，以及盾牌可能使子弹偏离目标的路径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers used a protected <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=target&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>target</strong></a> to compare how armour could deflect a bullet fired from a rifle or pistol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者用有防护的靶子，比较装甲如何偏转步枪或手枪射出的子弹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hit a target</span> · 击中目标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballistics</a></span> · 弹道学</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protective%20barrier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protective barrier</a></span> · 防护屏障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-024">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the museum, a diagram shows a pistol, a rifle and the path a shield might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deflect</strong></a> a bullet from its target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆的示意图展示了手枪、步枪，以及盾牌可能使子弹偏离目标的路径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers used a protected target to compare how armour could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deflect</strong></a> a bullet fired from a rifle or pistol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者用有防护的靶子，比较装甲如何偏转步枪或手枪射出的子弹。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The display includes a sword <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blade</strong></a>, an ax and a hammer from the same ancient workshop.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展品包括同一古代作坊的剑刃、斧头和锤子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A blacksmith used a hammer to shape the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blade</strong></a> of a sword or ax.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">铁匠用锤子锻造剑或斧头的刃。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservators cleaned the sword, checked the ax <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blade</strong></a> and repaired the hammer handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员清理了剑，检查斧刃，并修理锤柄。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor wore a helmet and carried a shield, while the display beside him held a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bow</strong></a>, an arrow and a spear.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员戴着头盔、拿着盾牌，旁边展柜放着弓、箭和矛。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical costume included a helmet and shield, but the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bow</strong></a>, arrow and spear remained on the exhibition wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史服装包括头盔和盾牌，而弓、箭和矛仍陈列在展墙上。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A museum label explains how a helmet and shield protected soldiers facing an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrow</strong></a> from a bow or a thrown spear.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆标签解释头盔和盾牌如何保护士兵抵挡弓射出的箭或投出的矛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical costume included a helmet and shield, but the bow, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrow</strong></a> and spear remained on the exhibition wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史服装包括头盔和盾牌，而弓、箭和矛仍陈列在展墙上。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">equip<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-039" aria-label="定位 equip">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-039" data-mark-label="equip" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="equip：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="equip：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 equip" title="在有道查询 equip">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.配备，装备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Agencies prepare volunteers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equip</strong></a> teams, provide supplies and deploy staff after floods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪灾后，机构培训志愿者、装备团队、提供物资并调派人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we deploy rescuers, we must prepare a route, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equip</strong></a> each team and provide communications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调派救援人员前，我们须准备路线、为各队配备装备并提供通信保障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will provide funds to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equip</strong></a> shelters, prepare residents and deploy emergency vehicles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政机构将提供资金，用于装备避难所、让居民做好准备，并调派应急车辆。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">equip a team</span> · 为团队配备装备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency%20response&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency response</a></span> · 应急响应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logistics</a></span> · 后勤</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-040">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.使预备好；为......做准备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Agencies <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prepare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prepare</strong></a> volunteers, equip teams, provide supplies and deploy staff after floods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪灾后，机构培训志愿者、装备团队、提供物资并调派人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we deploy rescuers, we must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prepare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prepare</strong></a> a route, equip each team and provide communications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调派救援人员前，我们须准备路线、为各队配备装备并提供通信保障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will provide funds to equip shelters, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prepare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prepare</strong></a> residents and deploy emergency vehicles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政机构将提供资金，用于装备避难所、让居民做好准备，并调派应急车辆。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prepare for emergencies</span> · 准备应对紧急情况</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency%20response&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency response</a></span> · 应急响应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logistics</a></span> · 后勤</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Agencies prepare volunteers, equip teams, provide supplies and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deploy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deploy</strong></a> staff after floods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪灾后，机构培训志愿者、装备团队、提供物资并调派人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deploy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deploy</strong></a> rescuers, we must prepare a route, equip each team and provide communications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调派救援人员前，我们须准备路线、为各队配备装备并提供通信保障。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will provide funds to equip shelters, prepare residents and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deploy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deploy</strong></a> emergency vehicles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政机构将提供资金，用于装备避难所、让居民做好准备，并调派应急车辆。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deploy resources</span> · 调配资源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency%20response&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency response</a></span> · 应急响应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logistics</a></span> · 后勤</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">hit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-043" aria-label="定位 hit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-043" data-mark-label="hit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hit" title="在有道查询 hit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v击，打；碰撞；打击；n.击，打；击中</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The trainer showed how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hit</strong></a> a pad, strike a bag and throw a punch without trying to beat an opponent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练示范如何击打护垫、拳袋以及出拳，而非试图打倒对手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the rehearsal, actors beat a drum, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hit</strong></a> a shield and strike poses before staging a harmless punch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">排练时，演员敲鼓、击盾并摆出姿势，随后表演一次无伤害的出拳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You should never <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hit</strong></a> a person, beat an animal or strike someone with a punch during an argument.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">争吵时，绝不应该打人、打动物或挥拳击打他人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hit the ball</span> · 击球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rehearsal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rehearsal</a></span> · 排练</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-control</a></span> · 自制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use a stable board to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chop</strong></a> vegetables, and do not stab at slippery food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">切菜要用稳固的案板，不要用力戳滑动的食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cooking instructor showed us how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chop</strong></a> herbs without using the knife to stab at the stems.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烹饪老师示范如何切香草，而不是用刀戳茎。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the stage scene, actors mime actions to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chop</strong></a> wood and stab a sack without using real blades.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">舞台场景中，演员不用真刀，模拟劈木和刺袋的动作。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.05｜冲突怎样影响生活与资源 {#group-18-05}

**串联方式：** 从困境、陷阱和阴谋进入贫困、饥荒、挨饿与剥夺，再看夺取与追逐。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">dilemma<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-049" aria-label="定位 dilemma">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-049" data-mark-label="dilemma" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dilemma：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dilemma：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dilemma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dilemma" title="在有道查询 dilemma">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (避退两难的)困境</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refugee plight posed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dilemma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dilemma</strong></a>, while rumours of a conspiracy created another pitfall for aid workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">难民的困境带来两难，而阴谋传言又给救援人员造成了另一风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A journalist investigating the conspiracy faced a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dilemma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dilemma</strong></a>: exposing it could worsen the plight of witnesses, a serious ethical pitfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查阴谋的记者面临两难：揭露它可能加剧证人困境，这是严重的伦理风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report examines the plight of families caught in a conspiracy, the official <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dilemma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dilemma</strong></a> and the pitfall of acting without evidence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告考察了卷入阴谋的家庭的困境、官员的两难及无证据行动的风险。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">face a dilemma</span> · 面临两难</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethical%20decision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ethical decision</a></span> · 伦理决策</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humanitarian%20crisis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humanitarian crisis</a></span> · 人道危机</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A journalist investigating the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conspiracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conspiracy</strong></a> faced a dilemma: exposing it could worsen the plight of witnesses, a serious ethical pitfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查阴谋的记者面临两难：揭露它可能加剧证人困境，这是严重的伦理风险。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">destitute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-053" aria-label="定位 destitute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-053" data-mark-label="destitute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="destitute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="destitute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 destitute" title="在有道查询 destitute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.赤贫的，一无所有的；缺乏的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Famine can leave families <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destitute</strong></a> and deprive children of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饥荒会让家庭陷入赤贫，剥夺儿童食物，直至他们饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aid workers fear that famine will deprive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destitute</strong></a> communities of supplies and cause livestock to starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员担忧饥荒将使赤贫社区失去物资，并导致牲畜饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief effort aims to prevent famine, help <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destitute</strong></a> families and stop drought from continuing to deprive people of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援旨在防止饥荒、帮助赤贫家庭，并阻止干旱继续让人失去食物而挨饿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">destitute families</span> · 赤贫家庭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=food%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">food security</a></span> · 粮食安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=relief%20supplies&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">relief supplies</a></span> · 救援物资</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">famine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-054" aria-label="定位 famine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-054" data-mark-label="famine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="famine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="famine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 famine" title="在有道查询 famine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.饥荒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Famine</strong></a> can leave families destitute and deprive children of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饥荒会让家庭陷入赤贫，剥夺儿童食物，直至他们饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aid workers fear that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>famine</strong></a> will deprive destitute communities of supplies and cause livestock to starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员担忧饥荒将使赤贫社区失去物资，并导致牲畜饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief effort aims to prevent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>famine</strong></a>, help destitute families and stop drought from continuing to deprive people of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援旨在防止饥荒、帮助赤贫家庭，并阻止干旱继续让人失去食物而挨饿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prevent famine</span> · 防止饥荒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=food%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">food security</a></span> · 粮食安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=relief%20supplies&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">relief supplies</a></span> · 救援物资</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v .(使)挨饿，饿死</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Famine can leave families destitute and deprive children of food until they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=starve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>starve</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饥荒会让家庭陷入赤贫，剥夺儿童食物，直至他们饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aid workers fear that famine will deprive destitute communities of supplies and cause livestock to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=starve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>starve</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员担忧饥荒将使赤贫社区失去物资，并导致牲畜饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief effort aims to prevent famine, help destitute families and stop drought from continuing to deprive people of food until they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=starve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>starve</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援旨在防止饥荒、帮助赤贫家庭，并阻止干旱继续让人失去食物而挨饿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">starve to death</span> · 饿死</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">deprive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-056" aria-label="定位 deprive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-056" data-mark-label="deprive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deprive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deprive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deprive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deprive" title="在有道查询 deprive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.剥夺</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Famine can leave families destitute and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deprive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deprive</strong></a> children of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">饥荒会让家庭陷入赤贫，剥夺儿童食物，直至他们饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aid workers fear that famine will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deprive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deprive</strong></a> destitute communities of supplies and cause livestock to starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援人员担忧饥荒将使赤贫社区失去物资，并导致牲畜饿死。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief effort aims to prevent famine, help destitute families and stop drought from continuing to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deprive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deprive</strong></a> people of food until they starve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援旨在防止饥荒、帮助赤贫家庭，并阻止干旱继续让人失去食物而挨饿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deprive people of food</span> · 剥夺食物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=food%20security&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">food security</a></span> · 粮食安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=relief%20supplies&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">relief supplies</a></span> · 救援物资</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">seize<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-057" aria-label="定位 seize">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-057" data-mark-label="seize" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="seize：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="seize：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 seize" title="在有道查询 seize">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.抓住；夺取</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seize</strong></a> opportunities to pursue meaningful research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生应抓住开展有意义研究的机会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seize</strong></a> this chance to pursue a lasting peace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政机构必须抓住此次机会追求长久和平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She decided to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seize&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seize</strong></a> the scholarship opportunity and pursue a career in medicine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她决定抓住奖学金机会，追求医疗事业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seize an opportunity</span> · 抓住机会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ambition</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">pursue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-058" aria-label="定位 pursue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-058" data-mark-label="pursue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pursue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pursue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pursue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pursue" title="在有道查询 pursue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.追赶；追查；追求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students should seize opportunities to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pursue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pursue</strong></a> meaningful research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生应抓住开展有意义研究的机会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council must seize this chance to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pursue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pursue</strong></a> a lasting peace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政机构必须抓住此次机会追求长久和平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She decided to seize the scholarship opportunity and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pursue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pursue</strong></a> a career in medicine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她决定抓住奖学金机会，追求医疗事业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pursue a goal</span> · 追求目标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ambition</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>
</div>

### 词群 18.06｜威胁如何升级为侵犯与包围 {#group-18-06}

**串联方式：** 区分冒犯、侵入、侵犯和攻击，再描述挑战、围困、包围与封闭。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">threat<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-059" aria-label="定位 threat">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-059" data-mark-label="threat" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="threat：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="threat：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 threat" title="在有道查询 threat">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-059</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.威胁，恐吓</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aggressive language may offend neighbours and be perceived as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>threat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻击性语言可能冒犯邻居，并被视为威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guard asked the aggressive visitor not to offend staff or make a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>threat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警卫要求这位态度强硬的访客不要冒犯员工或发出威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A policy can address aggressive conduct without assuming that everyone who happens to offend poses a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=threat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>threat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策可以处理攻击行为，而不必假定每个无意冒犯的人都构成威胁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a serious threat</span> · 严重威胁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conflict%20resolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conflict resolution</a></span> · 冲突解决</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">offend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-060" aria-label="定位 offend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-060" data-mark-label="offend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="offend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="offend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 offend" title="在有道查询 offend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-060</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.冒犯；犯罪；违反</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aggressive language may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offend</strong></a> neighbours and be perceived as a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻击性语言可能冒犯邻居，并被视为威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guard asked the aggressive visitor not to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offend</strong></a> staff or make a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警卫要求这位态度强硬的访客不要冒犯员工或发出威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A policy can address aggressive conduct without assuming that everyone who happens to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offend</strong></a> poses a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策可以处理攻击行为，而不必假定每个无意冒犯的人都构成威胁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">offend a neighbour</span> · 冒犯邻居</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conflict%20resolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conflict resolution</a></span> · 冲突解决</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">aggressive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-061" aria-label="定位 aggressive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-061" data-mark-label="aggressive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="aggressive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="aggressive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggressive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 aggressive" title="在有道查询 aggressive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.侵略的；好斗的；积极进取的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggressive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Aggressive</strong></a> language may offend neighbours and be perceived as a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻击性语言可能冒犯邻居，并被视为威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guard asked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggressive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aggressive</strong></a> visitor not to offend staff or make a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警卫要求这位态度强硬的访客不要冒犯员工或发出威胁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A policy can address <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aggressive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aggressive</strong></a> conduct without assuming that everyone who happens to offend poses a threat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策可以处理攻击行为，而不必假定每个无意冒犯的人都构成威胁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">aggressive behaviour</span> · 攻击性行为</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conflict%20resolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conflict resolution</a></span> · 冲突解决</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">invade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-062" aria-label="定位 invade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-062" data-mark-label="invade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="invade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="invade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 invade" title="在有道查询 invade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-062</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.入侵，侵略；侵扰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The court considered whether drones <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invade</strong></a> privacy, trespass on property, intrude on family life and violate basic rights.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法院审议无人机是否侵犯隐私、擅入地产、干扰家庭生活及违反基本权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourists must not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invade</strong></a> private gardens, trespass on farms, intrude into homes or violate local rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客不得闯入私家花园、擅入农场、侵入住宅或违反当地规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners argue that surveillance may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invade</strong></a> personal space, intrude on privacy, trespass into private affairs and violate consent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倡导者认为，监控可能侵犯个人空间、干扰隐私、擅自涉足私事并违反同意原则。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">invade a country</span> · 入侵国家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consent</a></span> · 同意</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-063">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-063" class="vocabulary-word-title">trespass<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-063" aria-label="定位 trespass">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-063" data-mark-label="trespass" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trespass：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trespass：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trespass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trespass" title="在有道查询 trespass">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-063</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.非法侵入；n.罪过；非法侵入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The court considered whether drones invade privacy, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trespass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trespass</strong></a> on property, intrude on family life and violate basic rights.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法院审议无人机是否侵犯隐私、擅入地产、干扰家庭生活及违反基本权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourists must not invade private gardens, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trespass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trespass</strong></a> on farms, intrude into homes or violate local rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客不得闯入私家花园、擅入农场、侵入住宅或违反当地规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners argue that surveillance may invade personal space, intrude on privacy, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trespass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trespass</strong></a> into private affairs and violate consent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倡导者认为，监控可能侵犯个人空间、干扰隐私、擅自涉足私事并违反同意原则。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">trespass on land</span> · 擅闯土地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consent</a></span> · 同意</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourists must not invade private gardens, trespass on farms, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intrude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intrude</strong></a> into homes or violate local rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客不得闯入私家花园、擅入农场、侵入住宅或违反当地规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners argue that surveillance may invade personal space, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intrude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intrude</strong></a> on privacy, trespass into private affairs and violate consent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">倡导者认为，监控可能侵犯个人空间、干扰隐私、擅自涉足私事并违反同意原则。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourists must not invade private gardens, trespass on farms, intrude into homes or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>violate</strong></a> local rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客不得闯入私家花园、擅入农场、侵入住宅或违反当地规则。</p></li>
