<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">attack<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-066" aria-label="定位 attack">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-066" data-mark-label="attack" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="attack：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="attack：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 attack" title="在有道查询 attack">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.攻击，进攻；n.攻击，进攻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attack</strong></a> on the clinic and an assault on its staff created a major security challenge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对诊所的袭击和对员工的人身攻击造成了重大安全挑战。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The challenge is to prevent an online <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attack</strong></a> from leading to a physical assault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">挑战在于防止网络攻击演变为人身攻击。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police reviewed each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attack</strong></a> and assault as part of a wider challenge to public safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方审查每起袭击和人身攻击，将其视为公共安全面临的更广泛挑战的一部分。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an armed attack</span> · 武装袭击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prevention</a></span> · 预防</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">assault<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-067" aria-label="定位 assault">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-067" data-mark-label="assault" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="assault：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="assault：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 assault" title="在有道查询 assault">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.猛攻；(身体)侵犯；V.袭击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An attack on the clinic and an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assault</strong></a> on its staff created a major security challenge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对诊所的袭击和对员工的人身攻击造成了重大安全挑战。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The challenge is to prevent an online attack from leading to a physical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assault</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">挑战在于防止网络攻击演变为人身攻击。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police reviewed each attack and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assault</strong></a> as part of a wider challenge to public safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方审查每起袭击和人身攻击，将其视为公共安全面临的更广泛挑战的一部分。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a physical assault</span> · 人身攻击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prevention</a></span> · 预防</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=challenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>challenge</strong></a> is to prevent an online attack from leading to a physical assault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">挑战在于防止网络攻击演变为人身攻击。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police reviewed each attack and assault as part of a wider <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=challenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>challenge</strong></a> to public safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方审查每起袭击和人身攻击，将其视为公共安全面临的更广泛挑战的一部分。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">siege<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-069" aria-label="定位 siege">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-069" data-mark-label="siege" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="siege：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="siege：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=siege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 siege" title="在有道查询 siege">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n包围，围攻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Walls enclose the town, but troops can surround it during a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=siege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>siege</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">城墙围住城镇，但军队仍可在围攻时将其包围。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows how soldiers surround the fortress during a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=siege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>siege</strong></a> while walls enclose its central courtyard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示士兵如何在围攻期间包围堡垒，而城墙围住其中庭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">High barriers enclose the compound, while guards surround the gate in a scene resembling a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=siege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>siege</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高围栏围住院落，警卫包围大门，场景如同围困。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">under siege</span> · 被围困</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">surround<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-070" aria-label="定位 surround">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-070" data-mark-label="surround" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="surround：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="surround：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surround&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 surround" title="在有道查询 surround">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.包围；环绕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Walls enclose the town, but troops can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surround&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surround</strong></a> it during a siege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">城墙围住城镇，但军队仍可在围攻时将其包围。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows how soldiers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surround&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surround</strong></a> the fortress during a siege while walls enclose its central courtyard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示士兵如何在围攻期间包围堡垒，而城墙围住其中庭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">High barriers enclose the compound, while guards <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surround&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surround</strong></a> the gate in a scene resembling a siege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高围栏围住院落，警卫包围大门，场景如同围困。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">surround a building</span> · 包围建筑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blockade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">blockade</a></span> · 封锁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fortress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fortress</a></span> · 堡垒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">enclose<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-071" aria-label="定位 enclose">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-071" data-mark-label="enclose" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enclose：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enclose：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enclose" title="在有道查询 enclose">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.围住，包围；把……封入信封，随信附上</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Walls <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enclose</strong></a> the town, but troops can surround it during a siege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">城墙围住城镇，但军队仍可在围攻时将其包围。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model shows how soldiers surround the fortress during a siege while walls <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enclose</strong></a> its central courtyard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模型展示士兵如何在围攻期间包围堡垒，而城墙围住其中庭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">High barriers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enclose</strong></a> the compound, while guards surround the gate in a scene resembling a siege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高围栏围住院落，警卫包围大门，场景如同围困。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enclose a garden</span> · 围住花园</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blockade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">blockade</a></span> · 封锁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fortress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fortress</a></span> · 堡垒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.07｜让冲突停下来的不同方式 {#group-18-07}

**串联方式：** 由暂停、停止和终止进入反对、反制、威慑、禁止及预防；oppress 是压迫。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">pause<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-072" aria-label="定位 pause">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-072" data-mark-label="pause" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pause：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pause：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pause" title="在有道查询 pause">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.暂停，停顿；v.暂停，停顿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a brief <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pause</strong></a>, managers decided to halt production and cease operations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短暂停顿后，经理决定停止生产并停止运营。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pause</strong></a> in talks does not necessarily mean that negotiations will halt or cooperation will cease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">谈判暂停不一定意味着协商将终止或合作将停止。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The warning caused traffic to halt, workers to cease drilling and the site to fall into a temporary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pause</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警报使交通停下、工人停止钻探，工地暂时停顿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a brief pause</span> · 短暂停顿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspension&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">suspension</a></span> · 暂停</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resumption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resumption</a></span> · 恢复</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-073">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-073" class="vocabulary-word-title">halt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-073" aria-label="定位 halt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-073" data-mark-label="halt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="halt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="halt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=halt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 halt" title="在有道查询 halt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-073</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)停止，(使)停住；n.停止，停住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a brief pause, managers decided to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=halt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>halt</strong></a> production and cease operations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短暂停顿后，经理决定停止生产并停止运营。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pause in talks does not necessarily mean that negotiations will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=halt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>halt</strong></a> or cooperation will cease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">谈判暂停不一定意味着协商将终止或合作将停止。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The warning caused traffic to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=halt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>halt</strong></a>, workers to cease drilling and the site to fall into a temporary pause.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警报使交通停下、工人停止钻探，工地暂时停顿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">halt production</span> · 停止生产</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspension&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">suspension</a></span> · 暂停</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resumption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resumption</a></span> · 恢复</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-074">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-074" class="vocabulary-word-title">cease<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-074" aria-label="定位 cease">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-074" data-mark-label="cease" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cease：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cease：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cease" title="在有道查询 cease">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-074</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.终止，停止</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a brief pause, managers decided to halt production and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cease</strong></a> operations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短暂停顿后，经理决定停止生产并停止运营。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pause in talks does not necessarily mean that negotiations will halt or cooperation will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cease</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">谈判暂停不一定意味着协商将终止或合作将停止。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The warning caused traffic to halt, workers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cease</strong></a> drilling and the site to fall into a temporary pause.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警报使交通停下、工人停止钻探，工地暂时停顿。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cease operations</span> · 停止运营</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspension&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">suspension</a></span> · 暂停</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resumption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resumption</a></span> · 恢复</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-075">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-075" class="vocabulary-word-title">oppose<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-075" aria-label="定位 oppose">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-075" data-mark-label="oppose" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oppose：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oppose：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oppose" title="在有道查询 oppose">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-075</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.反对，抵制</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppose</strong></a> laws that oppress minorities, counter harmful claims and deter intimidation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民可以反对压迫少数群体的法律、反驳有害言论并威慑恐吓行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coalition aims to counter propaganda, deter violence and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppose</strong></a> leaders who oppress dissenters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟旨在反制宣传、阻止暴力，并反对压迫异议者的领导人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Independent media help <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppose</strong></a> efforts to oppress communities, counter misinformation and deter abuses of power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立媒体有助于反对压迫社区的企图、反制错误信息并遏制滥权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">oppose a policy</span> · 反对政策</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civil%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civil rights</a></span> · 公民权利</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dissent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dissent</a></span> · 异议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">oppress<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-076" aria-label="定位 oppress">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-076" data-mark-label="oppress" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oppress：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oppress：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oppress" title="在有道查询 oppress">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.压迫，压制；使压抑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens can oppose laws that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppress</strong></a> minorities, counter harmful claims and deter intimidation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民可以反对压迫少数群体的法律、反驳有害言论并威慑恐吓行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coalition aims to counter propaganda, deter violence and oppose leaders who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppress</strong></a> dissenters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟旨在反制宣传、阻止暴力，并反对压迫异议者的领导人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Independent media help oppose efforts to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oppress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oppress</strong></a> communities, counter misinformation and deter abuses of power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立媒体有助于反对压迫社区的企图、反制错误信息并遏制滥权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">oppress a minority</span> · 压迫少数群体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civil%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civil rights</a></span> · 公民权利</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dissent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dissent</a></span> · 异议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">counter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-077" aria-label="定位 counter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-077" data-mark-label="counter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="counter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="counter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 counter" title="在有道查询 counter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.抵消；反击，反驳；adv.相反地；n.柜台；工作台</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens can oppose laws that oppress minorities, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counter</strong></a> harmful claims and deter intimidation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民可以反对压迫少数群体的法律、反驳有害言论并威慑恐吓行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coalition aims to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counter</strong></a> propaganda, deter violence and oppose leaders who oppress dissenters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟旨在反制宣传、阻止暴力，并反对压迫异议者的领导人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Independent media help oppose efforts to oppress communities, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counter</strong></a> misinformation and deter abuses of power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立媒体有助于反对压迫社区的企图、反制错误信息并遏制滥权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">counter an argument</span> · 反驳论点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civil%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civil rights</a></span> · 公民权利</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dissent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dissent</a></span> · 异议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">deter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-078" aria-label="定位 deter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-078" data-mark-label="deter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deter" title="在有道查询 deter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.阻止，威慑住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens can oppose laws that oppress minorities, counter harmful claims and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deter</strong></a> intimidation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民可以反对压迫少数群体的法律、反驳有害言论并威慑恐吓行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coalition aims to counter propaganda, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deter</strong></a> violence and oppose leaders who oppress dissenters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟旨在反制宣传、阻止暴力，并反对压迫异议者的领导人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Independent media help oppose efforts to oppress communities, counter misinformation and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deter</strong></a> abuses of power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立媒体有助于反对压迫社区的企图、反制错误信息并遏制滥权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deter aggression</span> · 威慑侵略</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civil%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civil rights</a></span> · 公民权利</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dissent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dissent</a></span> · 异议</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">forbid<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-079" aria-label="定位 forbid">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-079" data-mark-label="forbid" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="forbid：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="forbid：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forbid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 forbid" title="在有道查询 forbid">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.禁止；阻止</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forbid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forbid</strong></a> smoking and prohibit open flames to prevent accidents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则禁止吸烟和使用明火，以预防事故。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Signs prohibit entry and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forbid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forbid</strong></a> parking near the gate to prevent obstruction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标志禁止进入，并禁止在门口附近停车，以防阻塞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forbid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forbid</strong></a> bullying, prohibit weapons and provide training to prevent violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可禁止霸凌、禁止携带武器，并提供培训来预防暴力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">forbid smoking</span> · 禁止吸烟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety%20policy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">safety policy</a></span> · 安全政策</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restriction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restriction</a></span> · 限制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">prohibit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-080" aria-label="定位 prohibit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-080" data-mark-label="prohibit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prohibit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prohibit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prohibit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prohibit" title="在有道查询 prohibit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.禁止，不允许</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules forbid smoking and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prohibit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prohibit</strong></a> open flames to prevent accidents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则禁止吸烟和使用明火，以预防事故。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Signs <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prohibit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prohibit</strong></a> entry and forbid parking near the gate to prevent obstruction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标志禁止进入，并禁止在门口附近停车，以防阻塞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools may forbid bullying, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prohibit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prohibit</strong></a> weapons and provide training to prevent violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可禁止霸凌、禁止携带武器，并提供培训来预防暴力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prohibit entry</span> · 禁止进入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety%20policy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">safety policy</a></span> · 安全政策</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restriction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restriction</a></span> · 限制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">prevent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-081" aria-label="定位 prevent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-081" data-mark-label="prevent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prevent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prevent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prevent" title="在有道查询 prevent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.预防，防止；阻止，阻碍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules forbid smoking and prohibit open flames to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prevent</strong></a> accidents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则禁止吸烟和使用明火，以预防事故。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Signs prohibit entry and forbid parking near the gate to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prevent</strong></a> obstruction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标志禁止进入，并禁止在门口附近停车，以防阻塞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools may forbid bullying, prohibit weapons and provide training to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prevent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prevent</strong></a> violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可禁止霸凌、禁止携带武器，并提供培训来预防暴力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prevent accidents</span> · 预防事故</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety%20policy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">safety policy</a></span> · 安全政策</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restriction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restriction</a></span> · 限制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.08｜立场冲突与责任证据 {#group-18-08}

**串联方式：** 辨认反抗、背叛、叛国者和爱国者，再用责备、指责及证据讨论责任。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">revolt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-082" aria-label="定位 revolt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-082" data-mark-label="revolt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="revolt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="revolt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 revolt" title="在有道查询 revolt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.叛乱；反抗；v.叛乱；反抗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolt</strong></a>, leaders accused rivals of treason and warned them not to betray the movement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起义期间，领导人指控对手叛国，并警告他们不要背叛运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolt</strong></a> whose supporters debate whether refusing orders would betray the nation or constitute treason.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一次起义，支持者争论拒绝命令会否背叛国家或构成叛国。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Historians disagree on whether the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolt</strong></a> amounted to treason or whether the rulers had chosen to betray the people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家对起义是否构成叛国，以及统治者是否选择背叛人民看法不一。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a popular revolt</span> · 民众起义</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loyalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">loyalty</a></span> · 忠诚</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uprising&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">uprising</a></span> · 起义</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">betray<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-083" aria-label="定位 betray">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-083" data-mark-label="betray" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="betray：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="betray：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=betray&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 betray" title="在有道查询 betray">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.背叛，出卖；(无意中）泄露，流露出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the revolt, leaders accused rivals of treason and warned them not to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=betray&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>betray</strong></a> the movement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起义期间，领导人指控对手叛国，并警告他们不要背叛运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a revolt whose supporters debate whether refusing orders would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=betray&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>betray</strong></a> the nation or constitute treason.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一次起义，支持者争论拒绝命令会否背叛国家或构成叛国。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Historians disagree on whether the revolt amounted to treason or whether the rulers had chosen to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=betray&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>betray</strong></a> the people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家对起义是否构成叛国，以及统治者是否选择背叛人民看法不一。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">betray a country</span> · 背叛国家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loyalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">loyalty</a></span> · 忠诚</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uprising&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">uprising</a></span> · 起义</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">treason<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-084" aria-label="定位 treason">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-084" data-mark-label="treason" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="treason：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="treason：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 treason" title="在有道查询 treason">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.叛国罪，通敌罪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the revolt, leaders accused rivals of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treason</strong></a> and warned them not to betray the movement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起义期间，领导人指控对手叛国，并警告他们不要背叛运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a revolt whose supporters debate whether refusing orders would betray the nation or constitute <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treason</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一次起义，支持者争论拒绝命令会否背叛国家或构成叛国。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Historians disagree on whether the revolt amounted to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treason</strong></a> or whether the rulers had chosen to betray the people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家对起义是否构成叛国，以及统治者是否选择背叛人民看法不一。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">commit treason</span> · 叛国</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loyalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">loyalty</a></span> · 忠诚</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uprising&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">uprising</a></span> · 起义</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">rebel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-085" aria-label="定位 rebel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-085" data-mark-label="rebel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rebel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rebel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rebel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rebel" title="在有道查询 rebel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.反叛者；叛逆者；v.造反；反抗，反对</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One historian calls him a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rebel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rebel</strong></a>, another a traitor and a third a patriot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位历史学家称他为反叛者，另一位称他为叛徒，第三位称他为爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rebel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rebel</strong></a> refused the label of traitor and insisted he was a patriot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位反叛者拒绝接受叛徒标签，坚持说自己是爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rebel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rebel</strong></a> is remembered as a traitor or a patriot often depends on who writes the history.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">反叛者被记作叛徒还是爱国者，往往取决于由谁书写历史。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rebel leader</span> · 反叛领袖</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perspective</a></span> · 视角</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegiance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegiance</a></span> · 效忠</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-086">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-086" class="vocabulary-word-title">traitor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-086" aria-label="定位 traitor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-086" data-mark-label="traitor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="traitor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="traitor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 traitor" title="在有道查询 traitor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-086</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.叛徒，卖国贼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One historian calls him a rebel, another a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traitor</strong></a> and a third a patriot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位历史学家称他为反叛者，另一位称他为叛徒，第三位称他为爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rebel refused the label of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traitor</strong></a> and insisted he was a patriot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位反叛者拒绝接受叛徒标签，坚持说自己是爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether a rebel is remembered as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traitor</strong></a> or a patriot often depends on who writes the history.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">反叛者被记作叛徒还是爱国者，往往取决于由谁书写历史。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">brand someone a traitor</span> · 把某人称为叛徒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perspective</a></span> · 视角</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegiance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegiance</a></span> · 效忠</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-087">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-087" class="vocabulary-word-title">patriot<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-087" aria-label="定位 patriot">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-087" data-mark-label="patriot" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="patriot：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="patriot：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patriot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 patriot" title="在有道查询 patriot">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-087</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.爱国者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One historian calls him a rebel, another a traitor and a third a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patriot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patriot</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位历史学家称他为反叛者，另一位称他为叛徒，第三位称他为爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rebel refused the label of traitor and insisted he was a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patriot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patriot</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位反叛者拒绝接受叛徒标签，坚持说自己是爱国者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether a rebel is remembered as a traitor or a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patriot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patriot</strong></a> often depends on who writes the history.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">反叛者被记作叛徒还是爱国者，往往取决于由谁书写历史。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a committed patriot</span> · 坚定的爱国者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perspective</a></span> · 视角</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegiance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegiance</a></span> · 效忠</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-088">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-088" class="vocabulary-word-title">blame<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-088" aria-label="定位 blame">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-088" data-mark-label="blame" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="blame：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="blame：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 blame" title="在有道查询 blame">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-088</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.责备，指责；n.(对坏事应负的)责任</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without reliable evidence or conclusive proof, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blame</strong></a> and reproach may be unfair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有可靠证据或确凿证明，责怪和谴责可能不公平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry sought evidence and proof before assigning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blame</strong></a> or expressing reproach.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查在归责或表示谴责之前寻求证据与证明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her tone of reproach suggested <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blame&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blame</strong></a>, but she offered no evidence that could serve as proof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的责备语气暗含归罪，但没有提供能充当证明的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accept the blame</span> · 承担责任</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-089">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-089" class="vocabulary-word-title">reproach<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-089" aria-label="定位 reproach">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-089" data-mark-label="reproach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reproach：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reproach：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reproach" title="在有道查询 reproach">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-089</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.责备，指责；v.责备，指责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without reliable evidence or conclusive proof, blame and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproach</strong></a> may be unfair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有可靠证据或确凿证明，责怪和谴责可能不公平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry sought evidence and proof before assigning blame or expressing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproach</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查在归责或表示谴责之前寻求证据与证明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her tone of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproach</strong></a> suggested blame, but she offered no evidence that could serve as proof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的责备语气暗含归罪，但没有提供能充当证明的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tone of reproach</span> · 责备语气</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">evidence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-090" aria-label="定位 evidence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-090" data-mark-label="evidence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="evidence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="evidence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 evidence" title="在有道查询 evidence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-090</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.证据；根据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without reliable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evidence</strong></a> or conclusive proof, blame and reproach may be unfair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有可靠证据或确凿证明，责怪和谴责可能不公平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry sought <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evidence</strong></a> and proof before assigning blame or expressing reproach.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查在归责或表示谴责之前寻求证据与证明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her tone of reproach suggested blame, but she offered no <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evidence</strong></a> that could serve as proof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的责备语气暗含归罪，但没有提供能充当证明的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reliable evidence</span> · 可靠证据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">proof<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-091" aria-label="定位 proof">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-091" data-mark-label="proof" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="proof：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="proof：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proof&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 proof" title="在有道查询 proof">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.证明，证据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without reliable evidence or conclusive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proof&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proof</strong></a>, blame and reproach may be unfair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有可靠证据或确凿证明，责怪和谴责可能不公平。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry sought evidence and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proof&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proof</strong></a> before assigning blame or expressing reproach.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查在归责或表示谴责之前寻求证据与证明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her tone of reproach suggested blame, but she offered no evidence that could serve as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proof&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proof</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的责备语气暗含归罪，但没有提供能充当证明的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">conclusive proof</span> · 确凿证明</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.09｜人在动荡中的感受与反应 {#group-18-09}

**串联方式：** 从恐惧、警报和紧张进入混乱、情境、争夺与秩序；terrific 的常用褒义需单独辨认。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">terror<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-092" aria-label="定位 terror">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-092" data-mark-label="terror" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="terror：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="terror：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terror&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 terror" title="在有道查询 terror">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.恐惧，恐慌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The horrible sight filled residents with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terror&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terror</strong></a> and dread as an alarm warned of another terrible storm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">恐怖景象让居民充满惊恐与担忧，警报预示另一场可怕风暴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the terrible fire, even a minor alarm brought back the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terror&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terror</strong></a> and dread of that horrible night.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可怕火灾后，即使小警报也会唤起那个恐怖夜晚的惊恐与担忧。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The alarm sounded during a terrible storm, filling us with dread as horrible images of the flood brought back our earlier <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terror&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terror</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警报在可怕风暴中响起，洪水的恐怖画面唤起先前的惊恐，让我们满心恐惧。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in terror</span> · 惊恐地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fear</a></span> · 恐惧</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warning%20signal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">warning signal</a></span> · 警报信号</li></ul>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.可能发生的情况；剧情梗棚，脚本</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The worst-case <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scenario&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scenario</strong></a> for the contest was a reckless crowd disrupting order on a festive occasion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛最坏的情形是鲁莽人群在欢庆场合扰乱秩序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this occasion, officials planned for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scenario&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scenario</strong></a> in which reckless fans might threaten order after the contest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次，官员预设了鲁莽球迷可能在赛后威胁秩序的情形。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A contest should be an occasion for fair play; a reckless decision can create a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scenario&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scenario</strong></a> where order breaks down.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛应成为公平竞技的场合；鲁莽决定可能造成秩序崩溃的局面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a worst-case scenario</span> · 最坏情形</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crowd%20control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crowd control</a></span> · 人群管理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sportsmanship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sportsmanship</a></span> · 体育精神</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occasion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occasion</strong></a>, officials planned for a scenario in which reckless fans might threaten order after the contest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次，官员预设了鲁莽球迷可能在赛后威胁秩序的情形。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The worst-case scenario for the contest was a reckless crowd disrupting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=order&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>order</strong></a> on a festive occasion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛最坏的情形是鲁莽人群在欢庆场合扰乱秩序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this occasion, officials planned for a scenario in which reckless fans might threaten <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=order&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>order</strong></a> after the contest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次，官员预设了鲁莽球迷可能在赛后威胁秩序的情形。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A contest should be an occasion for fair play; a reckless decision can create a scenario where <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=order&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>order</strong></a> breaks down.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛应成为公平竞技的场合；鲁莽决定可能造成秩序崩溃的局面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">maintain order</span> · 维持秩序</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crowd%20control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crowd control</a></span> · 人群管理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sportsmanship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sportsmanship</a></span> · 体育精神</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.10｜竞争怎样被规划和限制 {#group-18-10}

**串联方式：** 先看进展、边界和控制，再辨竞争者、敌人、战略、战术与蓝图。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">progress<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-107" aria-label="定位 progress">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-107" data-mark-label="progress" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="progress：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="progress：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 progress" title="在有道查询 progress">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.前进；进步；进程</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To make <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>progress</strong></a> within the budget limit, we must rein in spending and respect the project's boundary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为在预算限额内推进，我们必须控制支出，并遵守项目边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agreement sets a boundary and a limit on patrols to rein in tensions and support <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>progress</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协议划定边界、限制巡逻，以控制紧张局势并支持进展。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A clear boundary helps rein in expansion, but an arbitrary limit may slow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>progress</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">清晰边界有助于控制扩张，但随意的限制可能拖慢进展。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make progress</span> · 取得进展</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restraint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restraint</a></span> · 克制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">negotiation</a></span> · 谈判</li></ul>
