<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agreement sets a boundary and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=limit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>limit</strong></a> on patrols to rein in tensions and support progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协议划定边界、限制巡逻，以控制紧张局势并支持进展。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A clear boundary helps rein in expansion, but an arbitrary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=limit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>limit</strong></a> may slow progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">清晰边界有助于控制扩张，但随意的限制可能拖慢进展。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agreement sets a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boundary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boundary</strong></a> and a limit on patrols to rein in tensions and support progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协议划定边界、限制巡逻，以控制紧张局势并支持进展。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">compete<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-111" aria-label="定位 compete">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-111" data-mark-label="compete" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="compete：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="compete：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 compete" title="在有道查询 compete">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-111</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.竞争；参加(比赛）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A competitor may contend for a title and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compete</strong></a> with a rival without treating anyone as an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">竞争者可以争夺冠军、与对手比赛，而不把任何人视为敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told each competitor to contend fairly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compete</strong></a> hard and regard a rival as a partner rather than an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练要求每位选手公平争夺、努力比赛，并把对手当作伙伴而非敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Businesses <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compete</strong></a> with a rival and contend for customers, but a competitor need not become an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">企业与对手竞争并争夺客户，但竞争者无须成为敌人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">compete for a prize</span> · 争夺奖项</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">competition</a></span> · 竞争</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair%20play&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fair play</a></span> · 公平竞技</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.竞争；争辩，主张</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A competitor may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contend</strong></a> for a title and compete with a rival without treating anyone as an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">竞争者可以争夺冠军、与对手比赛，而不把任何人视为敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told each competitor to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contend</strong></a> fairly, compete hard and regard a rival as a partner rather than an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练要求每位选手公平争夺、努力比赛，并把对手当作伙伴而非敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Businesses compete with a rival and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contend</strong></a> for customers, but a competitor need not become an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">企业与对手竞争并争夺客户，但竞争者无须成为敌人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">contend for a title</span> · 争夺冠军</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">competition</a></span> · 竞争</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair%20play&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fair play</a></span> · 公平竞技</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-113">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.竞争者，对手；v.与……匹敌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A competitor may contend for a title and compete with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rival</strong></a> without treating anyone as an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">竞争者可以争夺冠军、与对手比赛，而不把任何人视为敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told each competitor to contend fairly, compete hard and regard a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rival</strong></a> as a partner rather than an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练要求每位选手公平争夺、努力比赛，并把对手当作伙伴而非敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Businesses compete with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rival</strong></a> and contend for customers, but a competitor need not become an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">企业与对手竞争并争夺客户，但竞争者无须成为敌人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a close rival</span> · 实力相近的对手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">competition</a></span> · 竞争</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair%20play&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fair play</a></span> · 公平竞技</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.竞争者，对手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competitor</strong></a> may contend for a title and compete with a rival without treating anyone as an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">竞争者可以争夺冠军、与对手比赛，而不把任何人视为敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competitor</strong></a> to contend fairly, compete hard and regard a rival as a partner rather than an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练要求每位选手公平争夺、努力比赛，并把对手当作伙伴而非敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Businesses compete with a rival and contend for customers, but a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competitor</strong></a> need not become an enemy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">企业与对手竞争并争夺客户，但竞争者无须成为敌人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a strong competitor</span> · 强劲竞争者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">competition</a></span> · 竞争</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair%20play&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fair play</a></span> · 公平竞技</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-115">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.敌人，仇敌；敌兵，敌军</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A competitor may contend for a title and compete with a rival without treating anyone as an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enemy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enemy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">竞争者可以争夺冠军、与对手比赛，而不把任何人视为敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told each competitor to contend fairly, compete hard and regard a rival as a partner rather than an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enemy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enemy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练要求每位选手公平争夺、努力比赛，并把对手当作伙伴而非敌人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Businesses compete with a rival and contend for customers, but a competitor need not become an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enemy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enemy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">企业与对手竞争并争夺客户，但竞争者无须成为敌人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a common enemy</span> · 共同敌人</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">strategy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-116" aria-label="定位 strategy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-116" data-mark-label="strategy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="strategy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="strategy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strategy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 strategy" title="在有道查询 strategy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.策略；战略</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The blueprint sets out a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strategy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strategy</strong></a>, while each tactic and manoeuvre addresses a specific obstacle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">蓝图提出总体战略，而每项战术和行动处理具体障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strategy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strategy</strong></a> needs more than a clever tactic or manoeuvre; it needs a practical blueprint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功战略不仅需要巧妙战术或行动，还需要实用蓝图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Planners revised the blueprint after one tactic failed and a diplomatic manoeuvre changed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strategy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strategy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项战术失败、一次外交行动改变战略后，规划者修订了蓝图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a long-term strategy</span> · 长期战略</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implementation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">implementation</a></span> · 实施</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">tactic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-117" aria-label="定位 tactic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-117" data-mark-label="tactic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tactic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tactic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tactic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tactic" title="在有道查询 tactic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.策略，战术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The blueprint sets out a strategy, while each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tactic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tactic</strong></a> and manoeuvre addresses a specific obstacle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">蓝图提出总体战略，而每项战术和行动处理具体障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful strategy needs more than a clever <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tactic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tactic</strong></a> or manoeuvre; it needs a practical blueprint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功战略不仅需要巧妙战术或行动，还需要实用蓝图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Planners revised the blueprint after one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tactic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tactic</strong></a> failed and a diplomatic manoeuvre changed the strategy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项战术失败、一次外交行动改变战略后，规划者修订了蓝图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a negotiating tactic</span> · 谈判策略</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implementation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">implementation</a></span> · 实施</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-118">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful strategy needs more than a clever tactic or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manoeuvre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manoeuvre</strong></a>; it needs a practical blueprint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功战略不仅需要巧妙战术或行动，还需要实用蓝图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Planners revised the blueprint after one tactic failed and a diplomatic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manoeuvre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manoeuvre</strong></a> changed the strategy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项战术失败、一次外交行动改变战略后，规划者修订了蓝图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a diplomatic manoeuvre</span> · 外交行动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implementation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">implementation</a></span> · 实施</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">blueprint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-119" aria-label="定位 blueprint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-119" data-mark-label="blueprint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="blueprint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="blueprint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blueprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 blueprint" title="在有道查询 blueprint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-119</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.蓝图；行动计划</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blueprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blueprint</strong></a> sets out a strategy, while each tactic and manoeuvre addresses a specific obstacle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">蓝图提出总体战略，而每项战术和行动处理具体障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A successful strategy needs more than a clever tactic or manoeuvre; it needs a practical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blueprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blueprint</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">成功战略不仅需要巧妙战术或行动，还需要实用蓝图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Planners revised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blueprint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blueprint</strong></a> after one tactic failed and a diplomatic manoeuvre changed the strategy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一项战术失败、一次外交行动改变战略后，规划者修订了蓝图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a blueprint for reform</span> · 改革蓝图</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implementation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">implementation</a></span> · 实施</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.11｜警告、强制与力量变化 {#group-18-11}

**串联方式：** 从警告、迫使、抗议和反驳进入力量、增援、削弱、缓解及安慰。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-120">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-120" class="vocabulary-word-title">warn<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-120" aria-label="定位 warn">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-120" data-mark-label="warn" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="warn：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="warn：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 warn" title="在有道查询 warn">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-120</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.警告；告诫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Regulators <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>warn</strong></a> employers that safety rules compel and oblige them to protect staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管者警告雇主，安全规则强制并要求他们保护员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A notice may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>warn</strong></a> owners of a hazard, but a court order can compel repairs and oblige them to pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通知可以警告业主存在危险，但法院命令能强制维修并要求其付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>warn</strong></a> residents before laws compel evacuation and oblige families to leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法律强制撤离并要求家庭离开之前，官员必须警告居民。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">warn of danger</span> · 警告危险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legal%20duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legal duty</a></span> · 法律义务</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard</a></span> · 危险</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-121">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-121" class="vocabulary-word-title">compel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-121" aria-label="定位 compel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-121" data-mark-label="compel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="compel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="compel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 compel" title="在有道查询 compel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-121</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Regulators warn employers that safety rules <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compel</strong></a> and oblige them to protect staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管者警告雇主，安全规则强制并要求他们保护员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A notice may warn owners of a hazard, but a court order can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compel</strong></a> repairs and oblige them to pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通知可以警告业主存在危险，但法院命令能强制维修并要求其付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must warn residents before laws <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compel</strong></a> evacuation and oblige families to leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法律强制撤离并要求家庭离开之前，官员必须警告居民。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">compel compliance</span> · 强制服从</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legal%20duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legal duty</a></span> · 法律义务</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard</a></span> · 危险</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">oblige<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-122" aria-label="定位 oblige">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-122" data-mark-label="oblige" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oblige：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oblige：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oblige&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oblige" title="在有道查询 oblige">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.迫使，强迫；帮忙</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Regulators warn employers that safety rules compel and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oblige&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oblige</strong></a> them to protect staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管者警告雇主，安全规则强制并要求他们保护员工。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A notice may warn owners of a hazard, but a court order can compel repairs and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oblige&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oblige</strong></a> them to pay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通知可以警告业主存在危险，但法院命令能强制维修并要求其付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must warn residents before laws compel evacuation and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oblige&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oblige</strong></a> families to leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法律强制撤离并要求家庭离开之前，官员必须警告居民。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">oblige employers to act</span> · 要求雇主行动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legal%20duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legal duty</a></span> · 法律义务</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard</a></span> · 危险</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">protest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-123" aria-label="定位 protest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-123" data-mark-label="protest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="protest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="protest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 protest" title="在有道查询 protest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.抗议；申明；v.抗议；申辩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers used the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protest</strong></a> to refute allegations of public indifference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者利用抗议反驳公众冷漠的指控。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials tried to refute the claims that had prompted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员试图驳斥引发抗议的主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A peaceful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protest</strong></a> can challenge policy, but evidence is needed to refute a factual claim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">和平抗议可以挑战政策，但反驳事实主张需要证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a peaceful protest</span> · 和平抗议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demonstration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">demonstration</a></span> · 示威</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterargument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">counterargument</a></span> · 反论点</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">refute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-124" aria-label="定位 refute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-124" data-mark-label="refute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="refute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="refute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 refute" title="在有道查询 refute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-124</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.反驳，驳斥；否认</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers used the protest to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refute</strong></a> allegations of public indifference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者利用抗议反驳公众冷漠的指控。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials tried to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refute</strong></a> the claims that had prompted the protest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员试图驳斥引发抗议的主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A peaceful protest can challenge policy, but evidence is needed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refute</strong></a> a factual claim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">和平抗议可以挑战政策，但反驳事实主张需要证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">refute an allegation</span> · 驳斥指控</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demonstration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">demonstration</a></span> · 示威</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterargument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">counterargument</a></span> · 反论点</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-125">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-125" class="vocabulary-word-title">might<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-125" aria-label="定位 might">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-125" data-mark-label="might" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="might：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="might：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=might&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 might" title="在有道查询 might">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-125</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n力量，威力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Military <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=might&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>might</strong></a> and force may reinforce a government's position but impair public trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军事实力和武力可能巩固政府地位，却损害公众信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaders may use force to reinforce their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=might&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>might</strong></a>, even when doing so could impair diplomatic relations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人可能用武力增强实力，即使这会损害外交关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report asks whether economic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=might&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>might</strong></a> can reinforce cooperation without the force that would impair it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告探讨经济实力能否在不用破坏合作的强制力的情况下巩固合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">military might</span> · 军事实力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coercion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coercion</a></span> · 强制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trust</a></span> · 信任</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-126">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-126" class="vocabulary-word-title">force<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-126" aria-label="定位 force">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-126" data-mark-label="force" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="force：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="force：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=force&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 force" title="在有道查询 force">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-126</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n力量；武力；v.强迫，迫使；用力使......移动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Military might and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=force&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>force</strong></a> may reinforce a government's position but impair public trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军事实力和武力可能巩固政府地位，却损害公众信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaders may use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=force&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>force</strong></a> to reinforce their might, even when doing so could impair diplomatic relations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人可能用武力增强实力，即使这会损害外交关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report asks whether economic might can reinforce cooperation without the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=force&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>force</strong></a> that would impair it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告探讨经济实力能否在不用破坏合作的强制力的情况下巩固合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">use force</span> · 使用武力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coercion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coercion</a></span> · 强制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trust</a></span> · 信任</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-127">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-127" class="vocabulary-word-title">reinforce<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-127" aria-label="定位 reinforce">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-127" data-mark-label="reinforce" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reinforce：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reinforce：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reinforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reinforce" title="在有道查询 reinforce">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-127</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Military might and force may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reinforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reinforce</strong></a> a government's position but impair public trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军事实力和武力可能巩固政府地位，却损害公众信任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaders may use force to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reinforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reinforce</strong></a> their might, even when doing so could impair diplomatic relations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人可能用武力增强实力，即使这会损害外交关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report asks whether economic might can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reinforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reinforce</strong></a> cooperation without the force that would impair it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告探讨经济实力能否在不用破坏合作的强制力的情况下巩固合作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reinforce a bridge</span> · 加固桥梁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coercion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coercion</a></span> · 强制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trust</a></span> · 信任</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-128">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaders may use force to reinforce their might, even when doing so could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impair</strong></a> diplomatic relations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人可能用武力增强实力，即使这会损害外交关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report asks whether economic might can reinforce cooperation without the force that would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impair</strong></a> it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告探讨经济实力能否在不用破坏合作的强制力的情况下巩固合作。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-129">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-129" class="vocabulary-word-title">mitigate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-129" aria-label="定位 mitigate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-129" data-mark-label="mitigate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mitigate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mitigate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mitigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mitigate" title="在有道查询 mitigate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-129</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 减轻，缓和</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers offer comfort to survivors and help <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mitigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mitigate</strong></a> their suffering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者安慰幸存者，帮助减轻痛苦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better housing can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mitigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mitigate</strong></a> hardship and provide comfort after a disaster.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的住房能减轻困苦，并在灾后提供慰藉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Counselling aims to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mitigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mitigate</strong></a> distress and comfort bereaved families.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">心理辅导旨在减轻悲痛，并安慰丧亲家庭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mitigate suffering</span> · 减轻痛苦</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=support&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">support</a></span> · 支持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compassion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compassion</a></span> · 同情与关怀</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-130">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-130" class="vocabulary-word-title">comfort<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-130" aria-label="定位 comfort">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-130" data-mark-label="comfort" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="comfort：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="comfort：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comfort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 comfort" title="在有道查询 comfort">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-130</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.安慰；舒适；v.安慰，抚慰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers offer <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comfort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comfort</strong></a> to survivors and help mitigate their suffering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者安慰幸存者，帮助减轻痛苦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better housing can mitigate hardship and provide <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comfort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comfort</strong></a> after a disaster.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的住房能减轻困苦，并在灾后提供慰藉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Counselling aims to mitigate distress and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comfort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comfort</strong></a> bereaved families.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">心理辅导旨在减轻悲痛，并安慰丧亲家庭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">comfort survivors</span> · 安慰幸存者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=support&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">support</a></span> · 支持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compassion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compassion</a></span> · 同情与关怀</li></ul>
</div>

### 词群 18.12｜事件及其道德评价 {#group-18-12}

**串联方式：** 先客观说明事情发生，再用凶猛、残暴、邪恶等词表达带立场的评价。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-131">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-131" class="vocabulary-word-title">event<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-131" aria-label="定位 event">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-131" data-mark-label="event" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="event：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="event：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=event&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 event" title="在有道查询 event">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-131</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 事件，大事；公开活动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A rare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=event&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>event</strong></a> may happen unexpectedly, but similar incidents can occur so often that prevention becomes a serious matter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">罕见事件可能意外发生，但类似事故也可能频繁出现，使预防成为严肃问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=event&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>event</strong></a> does happen, officials must determine why failures occur and treat the matter seriously.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果事件确实发生，官员必须确定故障为何出现，并严肃处理此事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The matter concerns what might happen at a public <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=event&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>event</strong></a> and where crowding could occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此事涉及公共活动中可能发生什么，以及哪里可能出现拥挤。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a major event</span> · 重大事件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incident&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">incident</a></span> · 事件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contingency%20planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contingency planning</a></span> · 应急规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-132">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-132" class="vocabulary-word-title">matter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-132" aria-label="定位 matter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-132" data-mark-label="matter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="matter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="matter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=matter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 matter" title="在有道查询 matter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-132</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.事情；物质；v.要紧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A rare event may happen unexpectedly, but similar incidents can occur so often that prevention becomes a serious <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=matter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>matter</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">罕见事件可能意外发生，但类似事故也可能频繁出现，使预防成为严肃问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the event does happen, officials must determine why failures occur and treat the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=matter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>matter</strong></a> seriously.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果事件确实发生，官员必须确定故障为何出现，并严肃处理此事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=matter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>matter</strong></a> concerns what might happen at a public event and where crowding could occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此事涉及公共活动中可能发生什么，以及哪里可能出现拥挤。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a serious matter</span> · 严重事项</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incident&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">incident</a></span> · 事件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contingency%20planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contingency planning</a></span> · 应急规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">happen<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-133" aria-label="定位 happen">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-133" data-mark-label="happen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="happen：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="happen：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=happen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 happen" title="在有道查询 happen">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v发生，出现；碰巧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A rare event may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=happen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>happen</strong></a> unexpectedly, but similar incidents can occur so often that prevention becomes a serious matter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">罕见事件可能意外发生，但类似事故也可能频繁出现，使预防成为严肃问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the event does <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=happen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>happen</strong></a>, officials must determine why failures occur and treat the matter seriously.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果事件确实发生，官员必须确定故障为何出现，并严肃处理此事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The matter concerns what might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=happen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>happen</strong></a> at a public event and where crowding could occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此事涉及公共活动中可能发生什么，以及哪里可能出现拥挤。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">happen unexpectedly</span> · 意外发生</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incident&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">incident</a></span> · 事件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contingency%20planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contingency planning</a></span> · 应急规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If the event does happen, officials must determine why failures <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occur</strong></a> and treat the matter seriously.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果事件确实发生，官员必须确定故障为何出现，并严肃处理此事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The matter concerns what might happen at a public event and where crowding could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=occur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>occur</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此事涉及公共活动中可能发生什么，以及哪里可能出现拥挤。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-135">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.野蛮的，凶猛的 v，凶狠地攻击 n，野蛮人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the legend, a fierce devil inspires a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>savage</strong></a> attack involving foul play and an evil betrayal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">传说中，凶猛的魔鬼煽动了一次残暴袭击，其中涉及阴谋与邪恶背叛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The critic described the villain as an evil devil whose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>savage</strong></a> methods and foul motives met fierce resistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评论家把反派描述为邪恶魔鬼，其残暴手段与卑劣动机遭到激烈抵抗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The story portrays a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>savage</strong></a> ruler, fierce rebels, foul corruption and an evil adviser compared to a devil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">故事描绘了残暴统治者、强悍反叛者、卑劣腐败及被比作魔鬼的邪恶顾问。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a savage attack</span> · 残暴袭击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=villain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">villain</a></span> · 反派</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=folklore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">folklore</a></span> · 民间传说</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.13｜伤亡与纪念仪式 {#group-18-13}

**串联方式：** 从惩罚、处决、牺牲和伤亡进入埋葬、葬礼与墓地；hell 是宗教或比喻延伸。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-140">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-140" class="vocabulary-word-title">punishment<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-140" aria-label="定位 punishment">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-140" data-mark-label="punishment" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="punishment：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="punishment：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=punishment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 punishment" title="在有道查询 punishment">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-140</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.惩罚，处罚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical account examines capital <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=punishment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>punishment</strong></a> and the former practice of using a rope to hang and execute prisoners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份历史记录考察死刑及过去用绳索绞死、处决囚犯的做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers opposed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=punishment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>punishment</strong></a> that allowed the state to hang or otherwise execute offenders.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者反对允许国家绞死或以其他方式处决罪犯的刑罚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explains why some societies abolished the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=punishment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>punishment</strong></a> that authorised officials to hang and execute people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆解释了一些社会为何废除授权官员绞死和处决人的刑罚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">capital punishment</span> · 死刑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abolition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">abolition</a></span> · 废除</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=human%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">human rights</a></span> · 人权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-141">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-141" class="vocabulary-word-title">execute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-141" aria-label="定位 execute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-141" data-mark-label="execute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="execute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="execute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=execute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 execute" title="在有道查询 execute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-141</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.实行，执行；处死</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical account examines capital punishment and the former practice of using a rope to hang and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=execute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>execute</strong></a> prisoners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份历史记录考察死刑及过去用绳索绞死、处决囚犯的做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers opposed punishment that allowed the state to hang or otherwise <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=execute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>execute</strong></a> offenders.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者反对允许国家绞死或以其他方式处决罪犯的刑罚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explains why some societies abolished the punishment that authorised officials to hang and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=execute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>execute</strong></a> people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆解释了一些社会为何废除授权官员绞死和处决人的刑罚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">execute a prisoner</span> · 处决囚犯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abolition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">abolition</a></span> · 废除</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=human%20rights&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">human rights</a></span> · 人权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical account examines capital punishment and the former practice of using a rope to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hang&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hang</strong></a> and execute prisoners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份历史记录考察死刑及过去用绳索绞死、处决囚犯的做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers opposed punishment that allowed the state to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hang&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hang</strong></a> or otherwise execute offenders.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者反对允许国家绞死或以其他方式处决罪犯的刑罚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explains why some societies abolished the punishment that authorised officials to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hang&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hang</strong></a> and execute people.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆解释了一些社会为何废除授权官员绞死和处决人的刑罚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hang a prisoner</span> · 绞死囚犯</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-143">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-143" class="vocabulary-word-title">sacrifice<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-143" aria-label="定位 sacrifice">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-143" data-mark-label="sacrifice" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sacrifice：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sacrifice：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sacrifice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sacrifice" title="在有道查询 sacrifice">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-143</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.牺牲，献出；献祭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family had to bury another casualty of the war and mourn the needless <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sacrifice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sacrifice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人不得不安葬又一位战争死者，并哀悼无谓的牺牲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers helped bury each casualty, while survivors reflected on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sacrifice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sacrifice</strong></a> made by rescuers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助安葬每位死者，幸存者则追思救援者作出的牺牲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memorial honours a casualty whose family could not afford to bury him, recalling both hardship and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sacrifice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sacrifice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪念碑纪念一位家人无钱安葬的死者，追忆困苦与牺牲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a sacrifice</span> · 作出牺牲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remembrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">remembrance</a></span> · 纪念</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bereavement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bereavement</a></span> · 丧亲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">casualty<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-144" aria-label="定位 casualty">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-144" data-mark-label="casualty" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="casualty：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="casualty：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=casualty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 casualty" title="在有道查询 casualty">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-144</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.伤亡人员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family had to bury another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=casualty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>casualty</strong></a> of the war and mourn the needless sacrifice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家人不得不安葬又一位战争死者，并哀悼无谓的牺牲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers helped bury each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=casualty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>casualty</strong></a>, while survivors reflected on the sacrifice made by rescuers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助安葬每位死者，幸存者则追思救援者作出的牺牲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memorial honours a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=casualty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>casualty</strong></a> whose family could not afford to bury him, recalling both hardship and sacrifice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪念碑纪念一位家人无钱安葬的死者，追忆困苦与牺牲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a civilian casualty</span> · 平民伤亡者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remembrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">remembrance</a></span> · 纪念</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bereavement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bereavement</a></span> · 丧亲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers helped <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bury</strong></a> each casualty, while survivors reflected on the sacrifice made by rescuers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助安葬每位死者，幸存者则追思救援者作出的牺牲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memorial honours a casualty whose family could not afford to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bury</strong></a> him, recalling both hardship and sacrifice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪念碑纪念一位家人无钱安葬的死者，追忆困苦与牺牲。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-146">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.葬礼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=funeral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>funeral</strong></a>, the family visited a grave near an ancient tomb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">葬礼后，家人探访了古墓附近的一处坟墓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide distinguished the royal tomb from a simple grave and described the king's elaborate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=funeral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>funeral</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导游区分了王陵与普通坟墓，并描述了国王隆重的葬礼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A photograph of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=funeral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>funeral</strong></a> hangs beside a model tomb and a map showing the grave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">葬礼照片挂在陵墓模型和标有坟墓位置的地图旁边。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">attend a funeral</span> · 参加葬礼</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-147">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A photograph of the funeral hangs beside a model tomb and a map showing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grave</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">葬礼照片挂在陵墓模型和标有坟墓位置的地图旁边。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The naval fleet escorted a troop transport toward a landing site near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maritime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maritime</strong></a> trade route.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海军舰队护送运兵船前往海上贸易路线附近的登陆点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maritime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maritime</strong></a> museum displays a naval fleet model beside a troop transport used during the landing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海事博物馆在登陆行动使用过的运兵船旁陈列海军舰队模型。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The landing exercise involved a naval fleet and a troop carrier while keeping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maritime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maritime</strong></a> traffic clear.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登陆演习动用了海军舰队和运兵船，同时避开海上交通。</p></li>
</ol>
