<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.着陆；登陆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The naval fleet escorted a troop transport toward a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landing</strong></a> site near the maritime trade route.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海军舰队护送运兵船前往海上贸易路线附近的登陆点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A maritime museum displays a naval fleet model beside a troop transport used during the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landing</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海事博物馆在登陆行动使用过的运兵船旁陈列海军舰队模型。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landing</strong></a> exercise involved a naval fleet and a troop carrier while keeping maritime traffic clear.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登陆演习动用了海军舰队和运兵船，同时避开海上交通。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an emergency landing</span> · 紧急着陆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">navigation</a></span> · 航行</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transport%20vessel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transport vessel</a></span> · 运输船</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">hover<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-155" aria-label="定位 hover">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-155" data-mark-label="hover" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hover：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hover：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hover" title="在有道查询 hover">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.盘旋，悬浮；徘徊；处于不确定状态</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The military veteran watched a helicopter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hover</strong></a> as inspectors prepared to proceed with nuclear safety checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位退伍军人看着直升机盘旋，检查员准备继续进行核安全检查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A veteran of military rescue work advised the crew to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hover</strong></a> above the site before they proceed toward the nuclear facility.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有军事救援经验的老兵建议机组在现场上空盘旋，然后再向核设施前进。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The nuclear exercise required a military helicopter to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hover</strong></a> while a veteran coordinator decided how to proceed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">核应急演习要求军用直升机盘旋，由一名资深协调员决定如何继续行动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hover overhead</span> · 在上空盘旋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inspection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inspection</a></span> · 检查</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency%20exercise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency exercise</a></span> · 应急演习</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 18.15｜评估危险与破坏 {#group-18-15}

**串联方式：** 由有毒物质、风险、灾难和崩塌进入折磨、羞辱、损害及干扰。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-160">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-160" class="vocabulary-word-title">poison<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-160" aria-label="定位 poison">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-160" data-mark-label="poison" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="poison：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="poison：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 poison" title="在有道查询 poison">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-160</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.毒物，毒药；v.毒害</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A spill can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poison</strong></a> the river, devastate fishing communities and put livelihoods at risk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泄漏可能污染河流、重创渔业社区，并危及生计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials assessed the risk that leaking chemicals could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poison</strong></a> water and devastate farmland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员评估了化学品泄漏污染水源并毁坏农田的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reduce the risk, inspectors monitor substances that might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poison</strong></a> wildlife and devastate habitats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为降低风险，检查员监测可能毒害野生动物并摧毁栖息地的物质。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">poison a river</span> · 污染河流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=environmental%20damage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">environmental damage</a></span> · 环境破坏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-161">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-161" class="vocabulary-word-title">devastate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-161" aria-label="定位 devastate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-161" data-mark-label="devastate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="devastate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="devastate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devastate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 devastate" title="在有道查询 devastate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-161</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.毁坏，摧毁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A spill can poison the river, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devastate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devastate</strong></a> fishing communities and put livelihoods at risk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泄漏可能污染河流、重创渔业社区，并危及生计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials assessed the risk that leaking chemicals could poison water and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devastate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devastate</strong></a> farmland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员评估了化学品泄漏污染水源并毁坏农田的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reduce the risk, inspectors monitor substances that might poison wildlife and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devastate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devastate</strong></a> habitats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为降低风险，检查员监测可能毒害野生动物并摧毁栖息地的物质。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">devastate communities</span> · 摧毁社区</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=environmental%20damage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">environmental damage</a></span> · 环境破坏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-162">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-162" class="vocabulary-word-title">risk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-162" aria-label="定位 risk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-162" data-mark-label="risk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="risk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="risk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 risk" title="在有道查询 risk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-162</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.危险，风险阻冒着......的危险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A spill can poison the river, devastate fishing communities and put livelihoods at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>risk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泄漏可能污染河流、重创渔业社区，并危及生计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials assessed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>risk</strong></a> that leaking chemicals could poison water and devastate farmland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员评估了化学品泄漏污染水源并毁坏农田的风险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reduce the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>risk</strong></a>, inspectors monitor substances that might poison wildlife and devastate habitats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为降低风险，检查员监测可能毒害野生动物并摧毁栖息地的物质。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at risk</span> · 处于危险中</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contamination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contamination</a></span> · 污染</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=environmental%20damage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">environmental damage</a></span> · 环境破坏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-163">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-163" class="vocabulary-word-title">adventure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-163" aria-label="定位 adventure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-163" data-mark-label="adventure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="adventure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="adventure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 adventure" title="在有道查询 adventure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-163</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.冒险，冒险经历v，去(某地)探险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adventure</strong></a> turned into a catastrophe when a bridge collapse left hikers in peril.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">桥梁坍塌使徒步者陷入险境，探险变成了灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A responsible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adventure</strong></a> company prepares for peril, including a possible collapse or weather catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">负责任的探险公司会为险情做好准备，包括可能发生的坍塌或天气灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adventure</strong></a> interrupted by a mine collapse, showing how rescuers faced peril after the catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影讲述了一场被矿井坍塌打断的探险，展现救援者在灾难后如何面对险境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an outdoor adventure</span> · 户外探险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard%20assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard assessment</a></span> · 危险评估</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-164">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-164" class="vocabulary-word-title">peril<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-164" aria-label="定位 peril">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-164" data-mark-label="peril" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="peril：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="peril：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peril&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 peril" title="在有道查询 peril">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-164</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.重大危险；险情，危险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adventure turned into a catastrophe when a bridge collapse left hikers in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peril&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peril</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">桥梁坍塌使徒步者陷入险境，探险变成了灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A responsible adventure company prepares for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peril&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peril</strong></a>, including a possible collapse or weather catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">负责任的探险公司会为险情做好准备，包括可能发生的坍塌或天气灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows an adventure interrupted by a mine collapse, showing how rescuers faced <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peril&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peril</strong></a> after the catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影讲述了一场被矿井坍塌打断的探险，展现救援者在灾难后如何面对险境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in peril</span> · 处于险境</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard%20assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard assessment</a></span> · 危险评估</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-165">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-165" class="vocabulary-word-title">catastrophe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-165" aria-label="定位 catastrophe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-165" data-mark-label="catastrophe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="catastrophe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="catastrophe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catastrophe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 catastrophe" title="在有道查询 catastrophe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-165</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.重大灾难，大祸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adventure turned into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catastrophe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catastrophe</strong></a> when a bridge collapse left hikers in peril.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">桥梁坍塌使徒步者陷入险境，探险变成了灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A responsible adventure company prepares for peril, including a possible collapse or weather <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catastrophe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catastrophe</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">负责任的探险公司会为险情做好准备，包括可能发生的坍塌或天气灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows an adventure interrupted by a mine collapse, showing how rescuers faced peril after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catastrophe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catastrophe</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影讲述了一场被矿井坍塌打断的探险，展现救援者在灾难后如何面对险境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a natural catastrophe</span> · 自然灾难</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard%20assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hazard assessment</a></span> · 危险评估</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-166">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.倒塌；崩溃；v.倒塌；崩溃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adventure turned into a catastrophe when a bridge <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collapse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collapse</strong></a> left hikers in peril.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">桥梁坍塌使徒步者陷入险境，探险变成了灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A responsible adventure company prepares for peril, including a possible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collapse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collapse</strong></a> or weather catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">负责任的探险公司会为险情做好准备，包括可能发生的坍塌或天气灾难。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film follows an adventure interrupted by a mine <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collapse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collapse</strong></a>, showing how rescuers faced peril after the catastrophe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影讲述了一场被矿井坍塌打断的探险，展现救援者在灾难后如何面对险境。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a structural collapse</span> · 结构坍塌</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-167">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-167" class="vocabulary-word-title">torture<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-167" aria-label="定位 torture">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-167" data-mark-label="torture" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="torture：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="torture：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 torture" title="在有道查询 torture">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-167</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n拷问；痛苦，折磨 v拷问；使痛苦</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Abusive staff may humiliate detainees, cause lasting damage and engage in harmful treatment that amounts to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>torture</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">施虐员工可能羞辱被拘留者、造成持久伤害，并实施构成酷刑的有害待遇。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report condemns <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>torture</strong></a> and other harmful practices that humiliate people and damage their dignity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告谴责酷刑及其他羞辱人、损害尊严的有害做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rules must prevent officials from using <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=torture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>torture</strong></a> or harmful threats to humiliate suspects and damage their health.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则必须防止官员用酷刑或有害威胁羞辱嫌疑人并损害其健康。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prohibit torture</span> · 禁止酷刑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dignity</a></span> · 尊严</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">abuse</a></span> · 虐待</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-168">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-168" class="vocabulary-word-title">humiliate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-168" aria-label="定位 humiliate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-168" data-mark-label="humiliate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="humiliate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="humiliate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humiliate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 humiliate" title="在有道查询 humiliate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-168</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.羞辱，使蒙羞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Abusive staff may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humiliate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>humiliate</strong></a> detainees, cause lasting damage and engage in harmful treatment that amounts to torture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">施虐员工可能羞辱被拘留者、造成持久伤害，并实施构成酷刑的有害待遇。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report condemns torture and other harmful practices that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humiliate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>humiliate</strong></a> people and damage their dignity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告谴责酷刑及其他羞辱人、损害尊严的有害做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rules must prevent officials from using torture or harmful threats to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humiliate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>humiliate</strong></a> suspects and damage their health.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则必须防止官员用酷刑或有害威胁羞辱嫌疑人并损害其健康。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">humiliate a prisoner</span> · 羞辱囚犯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dignity</a></span> · 尊严</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">abuse</a></span> · 虐待</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-171">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-171" class="vocabulary-word-title">mislead<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-171" aria-label="定位 mislead">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-171" data-mark-label="mislead" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mislead：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mislead：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mislead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mislead" title="在有道查询 mislead">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-171</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.误导，欺骗，使误入歧途</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">False alerts can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mislead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mislead</strong></a> residents, disturb their sleep, interfere with work and interrupt essential services.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虚假警报可能误导居民、打扰睡眠、干扰工作并中断必要服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials asked reporters not to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mislead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mislead</strong></a> the public, disturb witnesses, interfere with evidence or interrupt rescue work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员要求记者不要误导公众、打扰证人、干扰证据或中断救援工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An intrusive advertisement may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mislead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mislead</strong></a> users, disturb their concentration, interfere with reading and interrupt a lesson.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">侵扰性广告可能误导用户、扰乱专注、妨碍阅读并打断课程。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mislead the public</span> · 误导公众</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disruption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disruption</a></span> · 干扰</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=misinformation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">misinformation</a></span> · 错误信息</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-172">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials asked reporters not to mislead the public, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disturb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disturb</strong></a> witnesses, interfere with evidence or interrupt rescue work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员要求记者不要误导公众、打扰证人、干扰证据或中断救援工作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An intrusive advertisement may mislead users, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disturb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disturb</strong></a> their concentration, interfere with reading and interrupt a lesson.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">侵扰性广告可能误导用户、扰乱专注、妨碍阅读并打断课程。</p></li>

### 词群 18.16｜撤离、让步与放弃 {#group-18-16}

**串联方式：** 比较后退、撤出、承受、妥协、投降、逃离和避免，再区分退出、放弃及丢弃。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-175">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-175" class="vocabulary-word-title">retreat<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-175" aria-label="定位 retreat">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-175" data-mark-label="retreat" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retreat：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retreat：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retreat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retreat" title="在有道查询 retreat">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-175</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.撤退；退避；v.撤退；退避</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unable to withstand pressure, the army began to withdraw, allowing its defensive line to shrink during the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retreat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retreat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军队无法承受压力，开始撤离，撤退期间防线缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan lets teams withdraw safely, withstand temporary setbacks and avoid letting their options shrink during a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retreat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retreat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划让团队安全撤离、承受暂时挫折，并避免在退让中选择范围缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As resources shrink, leaders must decide whether to withstand another crisis, withdraw staff or order a full <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retreat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retreat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资源减少时，领导人必须决定是再扛一次危机、撤走员工，还是下令全面撤退。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a strategic retreat</span> · 战略撤退</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=setback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">setback</a></span> · 挫折</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-176">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-176" class="vocabulary-word-title">shrink<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-176" aria-label="定位 shrink">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-176" data-mark-label="shrink" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shrink：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shrink：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shrink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shrink" title="在有道查询 shrink">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-176</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.退缩；收缩，缩水；(使)减少</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unable to withstand pressure, the army began to withdraw, allowing its defensive line to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shrink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shrink</strong></a> during the retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军队无法承受压力，开始撤离，撤退期间防线缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan lets teams withdraw safely, withstand temporary setbacks and avoid letting their options <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shrink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shrink</strong></a> during a retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划让团队安全撤离、承受暂时挫折，并避免在退让中选择范围缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As resources <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shrink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shrink</strong></a>, leaders must decide whether to withstand another crisis, withdraw staff or order a full retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资源减少时，领导人必须决定是再扛一次危机、撤走员工，还是下令全面撤退。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shrink in size</span> · 缩小</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=setback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">setback</a></span> · 挫折</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-177">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-177" class="vocabulary-word-title">withdraw<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-177" aria-label="定位 withdraw">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-177" data-mark-label="withdraw" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="withdraw：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="withdraw：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withdraw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 withdraw" title="在有道查询 withdraw">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-177</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.收回；(使)撤退；提（款)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unable to withstand pressure, the army began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withdraw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withdraw</strong></a>, allowing its defensive line to shrink during the retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军队无法承受压力，开始撤离，撤退期间防线缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan lets teams <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withdraw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withdraw</strong></a> safely, withstand temporary setbacks and avoid letting their options shrink during a retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划让团队安全撤离、承受暂时挫折，并避免在退让中选择范围缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As resources shrink, leaders must decide whether to withstand another crisis, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withdraw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withdraw</strong></a> staff or order a full retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资源减少时，领导人必须决定是再扛一次危机、撤走员工，还是下令全面撤退。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">withdraw troops</span> · 撤军</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=setback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">setback</a></span> · 挫折</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-178">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-178" class="vocabulary-word-title">withstand<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-178" aria-label="定位 withstand">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-178" data-mark-label="withstand" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="withstand：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="withstand：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withstand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 withstand" title="在有道查询 withstand">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-178</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.承受，顶住，经受住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unable to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withstand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withstand</strong></a> pressure, the army began to withdraw, allowing its defensive line to shrink during the retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">军队无法承受压力，开始撤离，撤退期间防线缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan lets teams withdraw safely, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withstand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withstand</strong></a> temporary setbacks and avoid letting their options shrink during a retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">计划让团队安全撤离、承受暂时挫折，并避免在退让中选择范围缩小。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As resources shrink, leaders must decide whether to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=withstand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>withstand</strong></a> another crisis, withdraw staff or order a full retreat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资源减少时，领导人必须决定是再扛一次危机、撤走员工，还是下令全面撤退。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">withstand pressure</span> · 承受压力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=setback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">setback</a></span> · 挫折</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-179">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-179" class="vocabulary-word-title">compromise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-179" aria-label="定位 compromise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-179" data-mark-label="compromise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="compromise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="compromise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 compromise" title="在有道查询 compromise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-179</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.妥协</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compromise</strong></a> can help both sides avoid conflict without forcing either to surrender or escape abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">妥协能帮助双方避免冲突，而不迫使任何一方投降或逃往国外。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The leaders sought a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compromise</strong></a> to avoid further fighting and let civilians escape before any surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人寻求妥协以避免更多战斗，并让平民在任何投降之前逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To escape the deadlock, negotiators proposed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compromise</strong></a> that would avoid a demand for total surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为摆脱僵局，谈判者提出了一个避免要求完全投降的妥协方案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reach a compromise</span> · 达成妥协</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiated%20settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">negotiated settlement</a></span> · 谈判解决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deadlock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deadlock</a></span> · 僵局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-180">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-180" class="vocabulary-word-title">surrender<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-180" aria-label="定位 surrender">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-180" data-mark-label="surrender" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="surrender：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="surrender：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surrender&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 surrender" title="在有道查询 surrender">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-180</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.投降；放弃；v.投降；(被迫）放弃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A compromise can help both sides avoid conflict without forcing either to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surrender&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surrender</strong></a> or escape abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">妥协能帮助双方避免冲突，而不迫使任何一方投降或逃往国外。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The leaders sought a compromise to avoid further fighting and let civilians escape before any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surrender&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surrender</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人寻求妥协以避免更多战斗，并让平民在任何投降之前逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To escape the deadlock, negotiators proposed a compromise that would avoid a demand for total <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surrender&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surrender</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为摆脱僵局，谈判者提出了一个避免要求完全投降的妥协方案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">surrender control</span> · 交出控制权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiated%20settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">negotiated settlement</a></span> · 谈判解决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deadlock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deadlock</a></span> · 僵局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-181">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-181" class="vocabulary-word-title">escape<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-181" aria-label="定位 escape">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-181" data-mark-label="escape" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="escape：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="escape：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 escape" title="在有道查询 escape">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-181</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.逃跑；逃脱；逃避；v.逃跑；逃脱；逃避</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A compromise can help both sides avoid conflict without forcing either to surrender or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escape</strong></a> abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">妥协能帮助双方避免冲突，而不迫使任何一方投降或逃往国外。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The leaders sought a compromise to avoid further fighting and let civilians <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escape</strong></a> before any surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人寻求妥协以避免更多战斗，并让平民在任何投降之前逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escape</strong></a> the deadlock, negotiators proposed a compromise that would avoid a demand for total surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为摆脱僵局，谈判者提出了一个避免要求完全投降的妥协方案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">escape danger</span> · 脱离危险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiated%20settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">negotiated settlement</a></span> · 谈判解决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deadlock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deadlock</a></span> · 僵局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-182">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-182" class="vocabulary-word-title">avoid<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-182" aria-label="定位 avoid">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-182" data-mark-label="avoid" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="avoid：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="avoid：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avoid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 avoid" title="在有道查询 avoid">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-182</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v避免；躲开</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A compromise can help both sides <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avoid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avoid</strong></a> conflict without forcing either to surrender or escape abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">妥协能帮助双方避免冲突，而不迫使任何一方投降或逃往国外。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The leaders sought a compromise to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avoid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avoid</strong></a> further fighting and let civilians escape before any surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人寻求妥协以避免更多战斗，并让平民在任何投降之前逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To escape the deadlock, negotiators proposed a compromise that would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avoid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avoid</strong></a> a demand for total surrender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为摆脱僵局，谈判者提出了一个避免要求完全投降的妥协方案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">avoid conflict</span> · 避免冲突</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=negotiated%20settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">negotiated settlement</a></span> · 谈判解决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deadlock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deadlock</a></span> · 僵局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-183">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-183" class="vocabulary-word-title">quit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-183" aria-label="定位 quit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-183" data-mark-label="quit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="quit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="quit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 quit" title="在有道查询 quit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-183</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.放弃，停止；辞去；离开</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To get rid of waste, the team chose to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quit</strong></a> unnecessary meetings, abandon paper forms, forgo printed reports and discard broken equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为减少浪费，团队决定停止不必要的会议、放弃纸质表格、不再印报告，并丢弃损坏设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The policy helps firms get rid of hazards, discard unsafe stock, abandon outdated methods, forgo quick profits and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quit</strong></a> risky practices.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策帮助企业消除隐患、丢弃不安全库存、放弃过时方法、不谋求快钱，并停止高风险做法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quit</strong></a> the project and abandon your plans, consider whether you can discard weaker ideas, forgo some features and get rid of avoidable costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">退出项目并放弃计划前，考虑能否舍弃较弱想法、放弃一些功能，并消除可避免的成本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">quit a job</span> · 辞职</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prioritisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prioritisation</a></span> · 确定优先次序</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste%20reduction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">waste reduction</a></span> · 减少浪费</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you quit the project and abandon your plans, consider whether you can discard weaker ideas, forgo some features and get <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rid</strong></a> of avoidable costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">退出项目并放弃计划前，考虑能否舍弃较弱想法、放弃一些功能，并消除可避免的成本。</p></li>

### 词群 18.17｜从防御走向胜负与纪念 {#group-18-17}

**串联方式：** 由保卫、抵抗和攻克进入胜利，再用纪念物、奖章、荣誉、贡献和奉献讨论记忆。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-188">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-188" class="vocabulary-word-title">defend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-188" aria-label="定位 defend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-188" data-mark-label="defend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="defend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="defend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 defend" title="在有道查询 defend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-188</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.防御，保护；为......辩护</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The game asks players to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defend</strong></a> a city, safeguard supplies, resist attacks, defeat an opponent and capture a flag.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游戏要求玩家保卫城市、保护物资、抵御进攻、击败对手并夺旗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign aimed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defend</strong></a> villages, safeguard civilians, resist occupation, defeat the attackers and capture their headquarters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该行动旨在保卫村庄、保护平民、抵抗占领、击败袭击者并夺取其总部。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-193">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-193" class="vocabulary-word-title">victory<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-193" aria-label="定位 victory">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-193" data-mark-label="victory" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="victory：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="victory：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 victory" title="在有道查询 victory">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-193</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.胜利，成功</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her triumph was to conquer her fear of speaking, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victory</strong></a> that mattered more than the prize.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的成功在于克服演讲恐惧，这场胜利比奖品更重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climbers celebrated a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victory</strong></a>, but their real triumph was learning to conquer doubt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者庆祝成功，但真正的胜利是学会克服疑虑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To conquer a difficult subject takes patience, and each small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>victory</strong></a> contributes to the final triumph.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻克难学的科目需要耐心，每个小胜利都促成最终成功。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a decisive victory</span> · 决定性胜利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=triumph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>triumph</strong></a> was to conquer her fear of speaking, a victory that mattered more than the prize.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的成功在于克服演讲恐惧，这场胜利比奖品更重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climbers celebrated a victory, but their real <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=triumph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>triumph</strong></a> was learning to conquer doubt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者庆祝成功，但真正的胜利是学会克服疑虑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To conquer a difficult subject takes patience, and each small victory contributes to the final <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=triumph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>triumph</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻克难学的科目需要耐心，每个小胜利都促成最终成功。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a personal triumph</span> · 个人胜利</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her triumph was to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conquer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conquer</strong></a> her fear of speaking, a victory that mattered more than the prize.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的成功在于克服演讲恐惧，这场胜利比奖品更重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climbers celebrated a victory, but their real triumph was learning to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conquer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conquer</strong></a> doubt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者庆祝成功，但真正的胜利是学会克服疑虑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conquer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conquer</strong></a> a difficult subject takes patience, and each small victory contributes to the final triumph.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">攻克难学的科目需要耐心，每个小胜利都促成最终成功。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">conquer a fear</span> · 克服恐惧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li></ul>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone tablet beside the statue explains why the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monument</strong></a> was built.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雕像旁的石碑解释了纪念碑的修建原因。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monument</strong></a> includes a bronze statue and a tablet listing the volunteers' names.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪念碑包括一座铜像和一块列有志愿者姓名的石碑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservators repaired the tablet, cleaned the statue and restored the surrounding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monument</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员修复石碑、清理雕像，并修缮周围纪念建筑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a national monument</span> · 国家纪念碑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commemoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commemoration</a></span> · 纪念</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heritage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">heritage</a></span> · 遗产</li></ul>

<summary><h4 id="word-18-199" class="vocabulary-word-title">medal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-199" aria-label="定位 medal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-199" data-mark-label="medal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="medal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="medal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 medal" title="在有道查询 medal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-199</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.勋章，奖章</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The town held a reception in honour of the athlete's glorious victory and gold <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>medal</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">城镇举行招待会，庆祝运动员的辉煌胜利和金牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>medal</strong></a> represents a glorious achievement, and she accepted it as an honour for the entire team.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的奖章代表辉煌成就，她把它视为全队的荣誉而接受。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>medal</strong></a> alone cannot honour every glorious act of courage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">单一奖章无法表彰每一种光辉的勇敢行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">win a medal</span> · 赢得奖章</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recognition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recognition</a></span> · 认可</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The town held a reception in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honour</strong></a> of the athlete's glorious victory and gold medal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">城镇举行招待会，庆祝运动员的辉煌胜利和金牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her medal represents a glorious achievement, and she accepted it as an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honour</strong></a> for the entire team.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的奖章代表辉煌成就，她把它视为全队的荣誉而接受。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A medal alone cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honour</strong></a> every glorious act of courage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">单一奖章无法表彰每一种光辉的勇敢行为。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her medal represents a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glorious&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glorious</strong></a> achievement, and she accepted it as an honour for the entire team.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的奖章代表辉煌成就，她把它视为全队的荣誉而接受。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-202">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-202" class="vocabulary-word-title">contribute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-202" aria-label="定位 contribute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-202" data-mark-label="contribute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="contribute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="contribute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 contribute" title="在有道查询 contribute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-202</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 捐献；(为···)作贡献；(为书、报刊等)撰稿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We owe thanks to volunteers who devote their weekends to the project and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contribute</strong></a> their skills.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们应感谢把周末投入项目并贡献技能的志愿者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students who devote time to helping others <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contribute</strong></a> to the community they owe so much to.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">投入时间帮助他人的学生，为给予他们很多的社区作出了贡献。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I owe my mentors gratitude because they devote energy to teaching and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contribute</strong></a> far beyond their duties.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我感激导师，因为他们倾力教学，贡献远超职责要求。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">contribute to research</span> · 为研究作贡献</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=service&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">service</a></span> · 服务</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gratitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gratitude</a></span> · 感激</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devote</strong></a> time to helping others contribute to the community they owe so much to.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">投入时间帮助他人的学生，为给予他们很多的社区作出了贡献。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I owe my mentors gratitude because they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devote</strong></a> energy to teaching and contribute far beyond their duties.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我感激导师，因为他们倾力教学，贡献远超职责要求。</p></li>
