### 词群 18.18｜守卫、侦察与隐蔽 {#group-18-18}

**串联方式：** 用巡逻、警戒、伪装、障碍与地洞组织场景，再辨间谍、侦察员、将军和士兵。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-205">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-205" class="vocabulary-word-title">patrol<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-205" aria-label="定位 patrol">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-205" data-mark-label="patrol" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="patrol：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="patrol：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 patrol" title="在有道查询 patrol">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-205</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.巡逻，巡查；v.巡逻，巡查</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patrol</strong></a> used camouflage to guard the crossing behind a natural obstacle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">巡逻队利用伪装，在天然障碍后守卫渡口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A guard in camouflage guided the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patrol</strong></a> around an obstacle on the path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">身着迷彩服的警卫引导巡逻队绕过路上的障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dense vegetation provided camouflage but became an obstacle for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patrol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patrol</strong></a> sent to guard the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">茂密植被提供了掩护，却成了派去守卫车站的巡逻队的障碍。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a border patrol</span> · 边境巡逻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concealment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">concealment</a></span> · 隐蔽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surveillance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surveillance</a></span> · 监视</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="18-206">
<summary><h4 id="word-18-206" class="vocabulary-word-title">guard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-18-206" aria-label="定位 guard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="18-206" data-mark-label="guard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="guard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="guard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 guard" title="在有道查询 guard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">18-206</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.卫兵，警卫员；v.看守；守卫，保卫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The patrol used camouflage to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guard</strong></a> the crossing behind a natural obstacle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">巡逻队利用伪装，在天然障碍后守卫渡口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guard</strong></a> in camouflage guided the patrol around an obstacle on the path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">身着迷彩服的警卫引导巡逻队绕过路上的障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dense vegetation provided camouflage but became an obstacle for the patrol sent to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guard</strong></a> the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">茂密植被提供了掩护，却成了派去守卫车站的巡逻队的障碍。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">guard an entrance</span> · 守卫入口</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A guard in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Camouflage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>camouflage</strong></a> guided the patrol around an obstacle on the path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">身着迷彩服的警卫引导巡逻队绕过路上的障碍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dense vegetation provided <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Camouflage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>camouflage</strong></a> but became an obstacle for the patrol sent to guard the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">茂密植被提供了掩护，却成了派去守卫车站的巡逻队的障碍。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.地洞，地道；V.掘地洞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The general asked a scout and a soldier to inspect a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burrow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burrow</strong></a> where a spy might hide documents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">将军请一名侦察兵和一名士兵检查一个洞穴，间谍可能在那里藏文件。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The general doubted the spy until a scout and a soldier found a concealed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burrow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burrow</strong></a> near the camp.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">直到侦察兵和士兵在营地附近找到隐蔽洞穴，将军才相信间谍。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **prevent a conflict**：预防冲突。
- **pose a threat**：构成威胁；pose 为补充词。
- **reach a compromise**：达成妥协；reach 为补充词。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A conflict can create threats to safety and cause serious harm. People may respond with force, resistance or negotiation. When both sides seek a compromise, they may reduce tension and prevent further damage. Memorials can later honour those who died and remind communities of the consequences of violence.

**中文理解：** 冲突可能威胁安全并造成严重伤害。人们可能以武力、抵抗或谈判回应。双方寻求妥协时，可能缓和紧张并避免进一步损害。纪念物此后可以纪念逝者，并提醒群体记住暴力的后果。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 概述一场冲突的报道：起因、参与者、威胁、应对、后果和妥协。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：沙场争锋
我记得的词群：
每群想起的词或搭配：
今天的 3 个 takeaway：
我的 3—5 句英文：
看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```

**接到下一章：** 报道中的士兵、领导者和受害者都是社会角色，进入第 19 章，从公共事件转向人与人的关系。
